  <script>
    // Automatically hide sidebar on mobile when a link is clicked
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
          const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar a');
          navLinks.forEach(link => {
              link.addEventListener('click', () => {
                  if(window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                      document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('show');
                  }
              });
          });
      });
  </script>

  <script>
    // ==========================================
    // SECURITY: GLOBAL HTML SANITIZER
    // ==========================================
    function escapeHtml(unsafe) {
        if (!unsafe) return "";
        return unsafe.toString()
             .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
             .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
             .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
             .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
             .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
    }
    let DATA = {};
    // --- PAGINATION MEMORY ---
     
    let currentClientPage = 1;
    const CLIENTS_PER_PAGE = 30;
    function changeClientPage(direction) { currentClientPage += direction; render(); }

    let currentInvPage = 1;
    const INV_PER_PAGE = 30;
    let currentPayPage = 1;
    const PAY_PER_PAGE = 30;
    function changePage(direction) { currentInvPage += direction; render(); }
    function changePayPage(direction) { currentPayPage += direction; render(); }
  
    // BULLETPROOF BOOTSTRAP 5 FOCUS TRAP DEFEATER
    // This stops Bootstrap from stealing your cursor when SweetAlert is open
    document.addEventListener('focusin', function(e) {
        if (e.target.closest('.swal2-container')) {
            e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        }
    }, true);
    let charts = {};
    let userRole = sessionStorage.getItem('userRole') || 'none';
    const Toast = Swal.mixin({ toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 3000, timerProgressBar: true, didOpen: (toast) => { toast.addEventListener('mouseenter', Swal.stopTimer); toast.addEventListener('mouseleave', Swal.resumeTimer); } });

    
    // --- SECURE 2FA LOGIN SYSTEM ---
    window.doLogin = function() { 
        const pinText = document.getElementById('login-pin').value.trim();
        const selectedRole = document.getElementById('login-role').value;
        if(!pinText) return;
        
        showLoad();
        google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(resStr) { 
            hideLoad();
            const res = JSON.parse(resStr);
            if (res.valid && res.step === "otp_sent") { 
                // Hide PIN, Show OTP
                document.getElementById('step-1-pin').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('step-2-otp').style.display = 'block';
                
                // Mask the email for security (e.g., ve***@gmail.com)
                let emailSplit = res.email.split('@');
                let maskedEmail = emailSplit[0].substring(0, 2) + '***@' + emailSplit[1];
                document.getElementById('otp-email-display').innerText = maskedEmail;
                
                Swal.fire({icon:'success', title:'OTP Sent!', text:'Check your email for the 6-digit code.', timer: 2500});
            } else { 
                Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'Access Denied', text: res.message}); 
            } 
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(e) { 
            hideLoad(); Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'System Error', text: 'Network Error.'});
        })
        .requestLoginOTP(selectedRole, pinText);
    };

    window.verifyOTP = function() {
        const otpText = document.getElementById('login-otp').value.trim();
        const selectedRole = document.getElementById('login-role').value;
        if(!otpText) return;
        
        showLoad();
        google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(resStr) {
            hideLoad();
            const res = JSON.parse(resStr);
            if (res.valid) {
                sessionStorage.setItem('loggedIn', 'true'); 
                sessionStorage.setItem('loginTime', Date.now()); 
                sessionStorage.setItem('sessionToken', res.token); // <--- ADD THIS LINE
                document.body.classList.add('logged-in'); // <--- FIXES THE BLANK PAGE
                userRole = res.role; 
                sessionStorage.setItem('userRole', userRole); 
                document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.opacity = 0;
                setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.display = 'none', 300);
                applyRBAC();
                loadData(); 
            } else {
                Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'Verification Failed', text: res.message});
            }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(e) { 
            hideLoad(); Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'System Error', text: 'Network Error.'});
        })
        .verifyLoginOTP(selectedRole, otpText);
    };

    // --- ROLE BASED ACCESS CONTROL ---
    window.applyRBAC = function() {
        const roleDisplays = document.querySelectorAll('.fw-bold.text-dark');
        roleDisplays.forEach(el => {
            if(el.id === 'userRoleDisplay') el.innerText = userRole === 'admin' ? 'Admin User' : 'Staff User';
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('.admin-only').forEach(el => {
            if (userRole === 'admin') {
                el.classList.remove('admin-only'); 
            } else {
                el.style.display = 'none'; 
            }
        });
    };

    window.addEventListener('load', function() { 
        hideLoad(); 
        // Check Dark Mode Preference
        if(localStorage.getItem('erp_dark_mode') === 'true') document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');
        
        // SECURITY FIX: 12-Hour Auto-Logout Enforcement
        const loginTime = sessionStorage.getItem('loginTime');
        const isSessionValid = loginTime && (Date.now() - parseInt(loginTime) < 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

        if (sessionStorage.getItem('loggedIn') === 'true' && isSessionValid) { 
            document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.display = 'none'; 
            document.body.classList.add('logged-in'); // <--- FIXES THE BLANK PAGE ON REFRESH
            userRole = sessionStorage.getItem('userRole') || 'admin';
            applyRBAC();
            loadData(); 
        } else {
            sessionStorage.clear(); // Purge expired sessions instantly
        }
        
        try { 
            var tooltipTriggerList = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]')); 
            var tooltipList = tooltipTriggerList.map(function (tooltipTriggerEl) { return new bootstrap.Tooltip(tooltipTriggerEl); }); 
        } catch(e) {} 
        setInterval(function() { 
            try { document.getElementById('clock').innerText = new Date().toLocaleString(); } catch(e){} 
        }, 1000); 
    });
    // --- DARK MODE ENGINE ---
    function toggleDarkMode() {
        document.body.classList.toggle('dark-mode');
        const isDark = document.body.classList.contains('dark-mode');
        localStorage.setItem('erp_dark_mode', isDark);
    }

    function doLogout() { sessionStorage.clear(); google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(url){ window.top.location.href = url; }).getScriptUrl(); }

    function toggleSidebar() { 
        const sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        const ma = document.getElementById('main-area');
        const tn = document.getElementById('top-nav');
        const tm = document.getElementById('top-menu-items');
        sb.classList.toggle('closed');
        ma.classList.toggle('expanded');
        tn.classList.toggle('expanded');
        if (sb.classList.contains('closed')) { tm.style.display = 'flex'; } else { tm.style.display = 'none'; }
    }

    function showLoad() { const l = document.getElementById("loader-overlay"); l.style.display = "flex"; setTimeout(()=>l.style.opacity = 1, 10); }
    function hideLoad() { const l = document.getElementById("loader-overlay"); l.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(()=>l.style.display = "none", 300); }
    
    function loadData() { 
        showLoad();
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'); // Grab the token
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            hideLoad(); 
            try { 
                const parsed = JSON.parse(res); 
                if(parsed.status === "error") { Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'Data Error', text: parsed.message}); return; } 
                DATA = parsed.data; 
                if(!DATA) throw new Error("No Data Received"); 
                render(); 
                renderCFODashboard();
                if (typeof renderReconTable === "function") renderReconTable();
                if (typeof renderCharts === "function") renderCharts();
                populateDatalists(); populateFilters(); loadConfig(); 
                if(document.getElementById('view-cashbook').classList.contains('active')) renderCashBook();
                if(document.getElementById('view-reports').classList.contains('active')) {
                    if(document.getElementById('rep-sec-fintb').style.display !== 'none') renderFinancials();
                    if(document.getElementById('rep-sec-finbs').style.display !== 'none') renderFinancials();
                }
             } catch (e) { console.error(e); } 
        }).withFailureHandler(function(e) { 
            hideLoad(); 
            if(e.message && e.message.includes('Security Block')) {
                sessionStorage.removeItem('loggedIn');
                sessionStorage.removeItem('sessionToken');
                // FIX: Never use reload() in Apps Script, it causes the Empty URL bug!
                document.body.classList.remove('logged-in');
                document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.opacity = 1;
                document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
                document.getElementById('step-1-pin').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('step-2-otp').style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                Swal.fire({icon:'error', title:'Connection Error', text: e});
            }
        }).getAppData(token); // <--- SEND THE TOKEN
    }

    function hexToRgba(hex, alpha) {
        let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
        if (hex.length === 4) { r = "0x" + hex[1] + hex[1]; g = "0x" + hex[2] + hex[2]; b = "0x" + hex[3] + hex[3]; } 
        else if (hex.length === 7) { r = "0x" + hex[1] + hex[2]; g = "0x" + hex[3] + hex[4]; b = "0x" + hex[5] + hex[6]; }
        return "rgba("+ +r + "," + +g + "," + +b + "," + alpha + ")";
    }

    function renderCharts() { 
        if(!DATA || !DATA.dashboard) return;
        if (charts.state) charts.state.destroy();
        if (charts.client) charts.client.destroy(); 
        if (charts.site) charts.site.destroy(); 
        if (charts.aging) charts.aging.destroy(); 
        if (charts.month) charts.month.destroy(); 
        if (charts.gst) charts.gst.destroy();
        
        const cfg = DATA.config || {}; 
        const stateType = cfg.CHART_STATE_TYPE || 'bar'; const stateColor = cfg.CHART_STATE_COLOR || '#0dcaf0';
        const sortedState = Object.entries(DATA.dashboard.stateSales).sort(function(a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; });
        charts.state = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-state'), { type: stateType, data: { labels: sortedState.map(function(x) { return x[0]; }), datasets: [{ label: 'Sales', data: sortedState.map(function(x) { return x[1]; }), backgroundColor: stateColor, borderRadius: 6 }] }, options: { indexAxis: (stateType==='bar' ? 'y' : 'x'), plugins: { legend: { display: (stateType!=='bar'),position: 'bottom', } } } });
        
        const clientType = cfg.CHART_CLIENT_TYPE || 'bar'; const clientColor = cfg.CHART_CLIENT_COLOR || '#007aff'; const cs = {};
        DATA.invoices.forEach(function(i) { if (i.type === 'Tax Invoice' && i.status !== 'Cancelled') cs[i.company] = (cs[i.company] || 0) + i.total; });
        const sortedCS = Object.entries(cs).sort(function(a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; }).slice(0, 5);
        charts.client = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-client'), { type: clientType, data: { labels: sortedCS.map(function(x) { return x[0].length > 15 ? x[0].substring(0, 15) + '...' : x[0]; }), datasets: [{ label: 'Sales', data: sortedCS.map(function(x) { return x[1]; }), backgroundColor: (clientType === 'bar') ? clientColor : [clientColor, '#3b82f6', '#60a5fa', '#93c5fd', '#bfdbfe'], borderWidth: 1 }] }, options: { maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { display: (clientType !== 'bar'), position: 'right', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 10 } } } } } });
        
        const siteType = cfg.CHART_SITE_TYPE || 'bar'; const siteColor = cfg.CHART_SITE_COLOR || '#34c759'; const ssMap = {};
        DATA.invoices.forEach(function(i) { if (i.type === 'Tax Invoice' && i.status !== 'Cancelled') { const siteName = i.siteName || "Main Site"; ssMap[siteName] = (ssMap[siteName] || 0) + i.total; } });
        const sortedSS = Object.entries(ssMap).sort(function(a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; }).slice(0, 5);
        charts.site = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-site'), { type: siteType, data: { labels: sortedSS.map(function(x) { return x[0].length > 15 ? x[0].substring(0, 15) + '...' : x[0]; }), datasets: [{ label: 'Sales', data: sortedSS.map(function(x) { return x[1]; }), backgroundColor: (siteType === 'bar') ? siteColor : [siteColor, '#a7f3d0', '#6ee7b7', '#34d399', '#10b981'], borderRadius: 6, borderWidth: 1 }] }, options: { maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { display: (siteType !== 'bar'), position: 'right', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 10 } } } } } });
        
        const agingType = cfg.CHART_AGING_TYPE || 'bar'; const agingColor = cfg.CHART_AGING_COLOR || '#ff3b30';
        charts.aging = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-aging'), { type: agingType, data: { labels: ['0-30', '31-60', '61-90', '90+'], datasets: [{ label: 'Due Amount', data: [DATA.dashboard.aging["0-30"], DATA.dashboard.aging["31-60"], DATA.dashboard.aging["61-90"], DATA.dashboard.aging["90+"]], backgroundColor: (agingType === 'bar') ? [agingColor, '#ff9f0a', '#ffcc00', '#d97706'] : agingColor, borderRadius: 6 }] }, options: { plugins: { legend: { display: (agingType !== 'bar'),position: 'bottom', } } } });
        
        const salesType = cfg.CHART_SALES_TYPE || 'line'; const salesColor = cfg.CHART_SALES_COLOR || '#34c759'; const mk = Object.keys(DATA.dashboard.monthSales).sort();
        charts.month = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-month'), { type: salesType, data: { labels: mk, datasets: [{ label: 'Sales', data: mk.map(function(k) { return DATA.dashboard.monthSales[k]; }), borderColor: salesColor, backgroundColor: hexToRgba(salesColor, 0.15), tension: 0.4, fill: (salesType==='line') }] }, options: { plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } } } });
        
        const gstType = cfg.CHART_GST_TYPE || 'bar'; const gstColor = cfg.CHART_GST_COLOR || '#5856d6'; const gk = Object.keys(DATA.dashboard.monthGST).sort();
        charts.gst = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-gst'), { type: gstType, data: { labels: gk, datasets: [ { label: 'IGST', data: gk.map(function(k) { return DATA.dashboard.monthGST[k].igst; }), backgroundColor: gstColor }, { label: 'CGST', data: gk.map(function(k) { return DATA.dashboard.monthGST[k].cgst; }), backgroundColor: '#af52de' }, { label: 'SGST', data: gk.map(function(k) { return DATA.dashboard.monthGST[k].sgst; }), backgroundColor: '#ff2d55' } ] }, options: { scales: { x: { stacked: true }, y: { stacked: true } }, plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { family: 'Inter', size: 11 } } } } } });
    }

    function loadConfig() { 
        if (DATA && DATA.config) { 
            if(document.getElementById('adm-pin')) document.getElementById('adm-pin').value = DATA.config.ACCESS_PIN;
            if(document.getElementById('adm-user-pin')) document.getElementById('adm-user-pin').value = DATA.config.USER_PIN || ''; 
            if(document.getElementById('adm-folder')) document.getElementById('adm-folder').value = DATA.config.DRIVE_FOLDER;
            if(document.getElementById('adm-logo')) document.getElementById('adm-logo').value = DATA.config.LOGO_URL; 
            if(document.getElementById('adm-signature')) document.getElementById('adm-signature').value = DATA.config.SIGNATURE_URL;
            if(document.getElementById('adm-upi')) document.getElementById('adm-upi').value = DATA.config.UPI_ID || '';
            if(document.getElementById('setting-overdue')) document.getElementById('setting-overdue').value = DATA.config.OVERDUE_DAYS;
            if(document.getElementById('adm-freeze')) document.getElementById('adm-freeze').value = DATA.config.FREEZE_DAYS || 30;
            
            if (DATA.config.LOGO_URL) document.getElementById('sidebar-logo').innerHTML = '<img src="' + DATA.config.LOGO_URL + '" style="height:30px; margin-right:10px; border-radius:5px;"> ERP'; 
            
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-sales-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-sales-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_SALES_TYPE || 'line';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-sales-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-sales-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_SALES_COLOR || '#34c759';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-gst-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-gst-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_GST_TYPE || 'bar';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-gst-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-gst-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_GST_COLOR || '#5856d6';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-client-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-client-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_CLIENT_TYPE || 'bar';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-client-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-client-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_CLIENT_COLOR || '#007aff';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-site-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-site-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_SITE_TYPE || 'bar';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-site-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-site-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_SITE_COLOR || '#34c759';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-state-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-state-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_STATE_TYPE || 'bar';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-state-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-state-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_STATE_COLOR || '#32ade6';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-aging-type')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-aging-type').value = DATA.config.CHART_AGING_TYPE || 'bar';
            if(document.getElementById('adm-chart-aging-color')) document.getElementById('adm-chart-aging-color').value = DATA.config.CHART_AGING_COLOR || '#ff3b30';
        } 
    }

    // --- COPY EVERYTHING BELOW THIS LINE ---

    function quickWaInv(idx) { 
        const inv = DATA.invoices[idx];
        if(!inv || !inv.pdf || inv.pdf.includes('#')) {
            return Swal.fire('Error', "Generate PDF first before sharing.", 'warning'); 
        }
        
        // Find client phone number from DB
        const clientObj = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === inv.company && (c.site || "Main Site") === (inv.siteName || "Main Site"));
        let phone = clientObj ? clientObj.phone : "";
        
        let msg = `Hello ${inv.company},\n\nYour document ${inv.invNum} for ₹${(inv.total || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})} has been generated.\n\nYou can view securely here: ${inv.pdf}\n\nThank you,\nSai Durga`;
        
        let url = 'https://wa.me/';
        if(phone) {
            phone = String(phone).replace(/\D/g, ''); 
            if(phone.length === 10) phone = '91' + phone; 
            url += phone;
        }
        
        url += '?text=' + encodeURIComponent(msg);
        window.open(url, '_blank'); 
    }
    function downloadFinalAccounts() {
    showLoad();
    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
        hideLoad();
        const d = JSON.parse(res);
        if (d.status === 'success') {
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'PDF Generated!',
                text: 'Your Trading, P&L, and Capital Account is ready.',
                icon: 'success',
                confirmButtonText: 'View PDF'
            }).then(() => {
                window.open(d.url, '_blank');
            });
        } else {
            Swal.fire('Error', d.message, 'error');
        }
    }).generateFinalAccountsPDF(token);
}

    function renderCFODashboard() {
        try {
            if(!DATA.dashboard || !DATA.dashboard.cfo) return;
            const cfo = DATA.dashboard.cfo;

            // --- PREMIUM 4-BOX FLAT GRID ---
            const topRow = document.getElementById('cfo-forecast') ? document.getElementById('cfo-forecast').closest('.row') : null;
            if (topRow) {
                // Change all existing columns to 25% width (col-md-3)
                const cols = topRow.querySelectorAll('.col-md-4');
                cols.forEach(c => { c.classList.remove('col-md-4'); c.classList.add('col-md-3'); });
                
                // Inject the 4th Box (Net GST Liability) with Flat UI matching existing boxes
                if (!document.getElementById('cfo-gst-box')) {
                    topRow.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `
                        <div class="col-md-3 mb-3 mb-md-0" id="cfo-gst-box">
                            <div class="card border-0 shadow-sm h-100" style="border-left: 4px solid #8b5cf6 !important; background-color: #ffffff;">
                                <div class="card-body p-3">
                                    <h6 class="text-muted fw-bold mb-1" style="font-size:0.8rem;"><i class="bi bi-bank text-info me-1"></i>Net GST Liability</h6>
                                    <div id="cfo-gst-val" class="fs-3 fw-bold text-dark">₹0</div>
                                    <div id="cfo-gst-sub" class="small text-muted" style="font-size: 0.75rem;">Calculating...</div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `);
                }
            }

            // 1. Render Predictive Forecast
            const fcEl = document.getElementById('cfo-forecast');
            if(fcEl) fcEl.innerText = '₹' + parseFloat(cfo.forecast30).toLocaleString('en-IN', {maximumFractionDigits:0});

            // 2. Render ACP
            const acpEl = document.getElementById('cfo-acp');
            if(acpEl) {
                acpEl.innerText = cfo.acp + ' Days';
                if(cfo.acp > 60) acpEl.className = 'fs-3 fw-bold text-danger'; 
                else if(cfo.acp < 40) acpEl.className = 'fs-3 fw-bold text-success'; 
                else acpEl.className = 'fs-3 fw-bold text-warning'; 
            }

            // 3. Render Revenue Concentration
            const concEl = document.getElementById('cfo-conc');
            if(concEl) {
                concEl.innerText = cfo.concentrationPct + '%';
                if(cfo.concentrationPct > 60) concEl.className = 'fs-3 fw-bold text-danger'; 
                else concEl.className = 'fs-3 fw-bold text-dark';
            }

            // 4. Render Net GST Liability (Math Engine)
            let gstCollected = 0, itcPaid = 0;
            if(DATA.invoices) {
                DATA.invoices.forEach(i => {
                    if (i.status !== 'Cancelled' && i.type === 'Tax Invoice') {
                        gstCollected += parseFloat(i.igst || 0) + parseFloat(i.cgst || 0) + parseFloat(i.sgst || 0);
                    }
                });
            }
            if(DATA.purchases) {
                DATA.purchases.forEach(p => { itcPaid += parseFloat(p.tax || 0); });
            }
            let netGst = gstCollected - itcPaid;
            const gstValEl = document.getElementById('cfo-gst-val');
            const gstSubEl = document.getElementById('cfo-gst-sub');
            if (gstValEl && gstSubEl) {
                gstValEl.innerText = '₹' + Math.abs(netGst).toLocaleString('en-IN', {maximumFractionDigits:0});
                gstValEl.className = netGst > 0 ? 'fs-3 fw-bold text-danger' : 'fs-3 fw-bold text-success';
                gstSubEl.innerText = netGst > 0 ? '(Payable to Govt)' : '(ITC Credit Available)';
            }
            
            // 5. Render Top Clients List
            const sitesTbody = document.getElementById('cfo-sites-tbody');
            if(sitesTbody) {
                let sitesHtml = '';
                if(cfo.sites.length === 0) {
                    sitesHtml = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">No site data available yet.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    cfo.sites.forEach(s => {
                        let badgeClass = s.margin >= 30 ? 'bg-success' : (s.margin >= 15 ? 'bg-warning text-dark' : 'bg-danger');
                        sitesHtml += `
                        <tr>
                            <td><strong>${s.site}</strong></td>
                            <td class="text-end fw-bold">₹${s.rev.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td>
                            <td class="text-end text-danger">₹${s.cost.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td>
                            <td class="text-end"><span class="badge ${badgeClass} fs-6 py-2 px-3">${s.margin.toFixed(1)}%</span></td>
                        </tr>`;
                    });
                }
                sitesTbody.innerHTML = sitesHtml;
            }

            // 6. PREMIUM FIX: Render Top 3 Dependency Risk (Actual Pending Due Math)
            // FIXED: Pointing to the correct HTML ID "cfo-top-clients"
            const riskEl = document.getElementById('cfo-top-clients');
            if (riskEl) {
                let clientRisk = {};
                
                if(DATA.invoices) {
                    DATA.invoices.forEach(i => {
                        // STRICT RULE: Ignore Quotations, Cancelled, and fully Received invoices!
                        if(i.type === 'Tax Invoice' && i.status !== 'Cancelled' && i.status !== 'Received') { 
                            let billed = parseFloat(i.total || 0);
                            let paid = 0;
                            
                            if(DATA.payments) {
                                paid = DATA.payments.filter(p => p.ref === i.invNum).reduce((sum, p) => sum + parseFloat(p.amount || 0), 0);
                            }
                            let due = billed - paid;
                            
                            // Only add to risk if they actually owe money!
                            if (due > 0.5) {
                                // FIXED: Group by Client Company, not by Site
                                let cName = i.company && i.company.trim() !== "" ? i.company : "Unknown Client";
                                clientRisk[cName] = (clientRisk[cName] || 0) + due;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
                
                let sortedClients = Object.entries(clientRisk).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0,3);
                let riskHtml = '';
                
                if (sortedClients.length === 0) {
                    // Beautiful UI when there is no risk
                    riskHtml = `<div class="text-center text-success mt-3"><i class="bi bi-shield-check fs-1"></i><br><small class="fw-bold">0 Pending Risk</small></div>`;
                } else {
                    sortedClients.forEach((r, idx) => {
                        let rColor = idx === 0 ? 'text-danger' : 'text-warning';
                        riskHtml += `
                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom py-2 small">
                            <span class="text-muted fw-bold text-truncate" style="max-width:150px;" title="${r[0]}">${r[0]}</span>
                            <span class="${rColor} fw-bold">₹${r[1].toLocaleString('en-IN', {maximumFractionDigits:0})}</span>
                        </div>`;
                    });
                }
                riskEl.innerHTML = riskHtml;
            }

        } catch(e) {
            console.error("CFO Dashboard Error:", e);
        }
    }
// ==========================================
    // PURCHASE ORDER ENGINE
    // ==========================================
    function renderPOs() {
        if(!DATA.purchaseOrders) return;
        document.getElementById('tbl-po').innerHTML = DATA.purchaseOrders.map((p, idx) => {
            let b = 'bg-secondary';
            if(p.status === 'Issued' || p.status === 'Fully Received') b = 'bg-success';
            else if(p.status === 'Partially Received') b = 'bg-info text-dark';
            else if(p.status === 'Cancelled') b = 'bg-danger';

            const emailBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="quickEmailPO('${p.poNum}')"><i class="bi bi-envelope text-primary me-2"></i>Email Vendor</a></li>`;
            
            const menu = `<div class="dropdown"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border shadow-sm px-2" type="button" data-bs-toggle="dropdown"><i class="bi bi-three-dots-vertical"></i></button><ul class="dropdown-menu shadow-lg border-0 p-2" style="border-radius:16px;">${emailBtn}<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="editPO('${p.poNum}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil text-primary me-2"></i>Edit PO</a></li><li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="${p.pdf}" target="_blank"><i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger me-2"></i>View PDF</a></li>${userRole === 'admin' ? `<li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li><li><a class="dropdown-item text-danger fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="del('PurchaseOrders','${p.poNum}')"><i class="bi bi-trash me-2"></i>Delete</a></li>` : ''}</ul></div>`;

            return `<tr><td><span class="text-primary fw-bold font-monospace">${p.poNum}</span></td><td>${p.date}</td><td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">${p.vendor}</span></td><td>${p.site}</td><td class="fw-bold">₹${(p.total||0).toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td><td><span class="badge ${b} rounded-pill px-3">${p.status}</span></td><td class="text-end">${menu}</td></tr>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    function initPO() {
        showModal('modal-po'); document.getElementById('form-po').reset(); document.getElementById('po-items').innerHTML = "";
        
        // FIX: Sequential PO numbering from the Backend!
        document.getElementById('po-no').value = DATA.nextIds.PO || ("PO-" + Date.now().toString().slice(-6));
        document.getElementById('po-date').valueAsDate = new Date();
        document.getElementById('po-subject').value = "";
        
        document.getElementById('btn-save-po').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('po-actions').style.display = 'none';
        
        // Populate Vendors & Sites
        const vSel = document.getElementById('po-vendor'); vSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Vendor...</option>';
        if(DATA.vendors) DATA.vendors.forEach(v => vSel.add(new Option(v.name, v.name)));
        const sSel = document.getElementById('po-site'); sSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Site...</option>';
        const uniqueSites = [...new Set((DATA.clients||[]).map(c => c.site || "Main Site"))].sort();
        uniqueSites.forEach(s => sSel.add(new Option(s, s)));
        
        addPOItem();
    }

    function editPO(poNum) {
        const p = DATA.purchaseOrders.find(x => x.poNum === poNum);
        if(!p) return;
        showModal('modal-po'); document.getElementById('form-po').reset();
        
        document.getElementById('po-actions').style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('btn-save-po').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('po-pdf-link').href = p.pdf;
        
        document.getElementById('po-no').value = p.poNum;
        document.getElementById('po-date').value = p.date;
        document.getElementById('po-status').value = p.status;
        document.getElementById('po-subject').value = p.subject || "";
        
        const vSel = document.getElementById('po-vendor'); vSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Vendor...</option>';
        if(DATA.vendors) DATA.vendors.forEach(v => vSel.add(new Option(v.name, v.name)));
        vSel.value = p.vendor;
        document.getElementById('po-vendor-addr').value = p.vendorAddr;
        
        const sSel = document.getElementById('po-site'); sSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Site...</option>';
        const uniqueSites = [...new Set((DATA.clients||[]).map(c => c.site || "Main Site"))].sort();
        uniqueSites.forEach(s => sSel.add(new Option(s, s)));
        sSel.value = p.site;
        document.getElementById('po-ship-addr').value = p.shipAddr;
        
        document.getElementById('po-gstrate').value = p.taxRate;
        document.getElementById('po-notes').value = p.notes;
        document.getElementById('po-terms').value = p.terms;
        
        document.getElementById('po-items').innerHTML = "";
        try {
            const items = JSON.parse(p.items);
            items.forEach(it => {
                const tr = `<tr>
                    <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d" value="${it.desc}" required></td>
                    <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" value="${it.costHead}" required></td>
                    <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" value="${it.sac}" required></td>
                    <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" value="${it.uom}" required></td>
                    <td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" value="${it.qty}" oninput="calcPO()" required></td>
                    <td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" value="${it.rate}" oninput="calcPO()" required></td>
                    <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly value="${it.amount}"></td>
                    <td><button type="button" class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove();calcPO()"></button></td>
                </tr>`;
                document.getElementById('po-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr);
            });
        } catch(e) {}
        calcPO();
    }

    function fillPOVendor() {
        const vName = document.getElementById('po-vendor').value;
        const v = DATA.vendors.find(x => x.name === vName);
        document.getElementById('po-vendor-addr').value = v ? `GSTIN: ${v.gstin||'N/A'}\nPhone: ${v.phone||'N/A'}\nAddress: ${v.address||''}` : '';
    }

    function fillPOSite() {
        const sName = document.getElementById('po-site').value;
        const c = DATA.clients.find(x => (x.site || "Main Site") === sName);
        document.getElementById('po-ship-addr').value = c ? `${c.name}\n${c.address||''}\nState: ${c.state||''}` : '';
    }

    function addPOItem() {
        const tr = `<tr>
            <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d" placeholder="Item Name" required></td>
            <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" placeholder="Select..." required></td>
            <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" placeholder="HSN" required></td>
            <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" placeholder="Unit" required></td>
            <td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" oninput="calcPO()" required></td>
            <td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" oninput="calcPO()" required></td>
            <td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly></td>
            <td><button type="button" class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove();calcPO()"></button></td>
        </tr>`;
        document.getElementById('po-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr);
    }

    function calcPO() {
        let t = 0;
        document.querySelectorAll('#po-items tr').forEach(r => {
            const q = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.q').value) || 0; const rate = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.r').value) || 0;
            const a = (q * rate); r.querySelector('.a').value = a.toFixed(2); t += a;
        });
        const gstRate = parseFloat(document.getElementById('po-gstrate').value) || 0;
        const gstAmt = t * (gstRate/100);
        document.getElementById('po-subtotal').innerText = t.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
        document.getElementById('po-gst-amt').innerText = gstAmt.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
        document.getElementById('po-total').innerText = Math.round(t + gstAmt).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
    }

    function savePO(e) {
    e.preventDefault(); 
    showLoad();

    // 1. GET TOTAL GST VALUE
    const gstVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('po-gst-amt').innerText.replace(/[^\d.-]/g, '')) || 0;
    
    // 2. CHECK VENDOR STATE (Safely checks if you have a state field, defaults to AP if missing)
    let stateEle = document.getElementById('po-state'); 
    let vendorState = stateEle ? stateEle.value : "Andhra Pradesh"; 

    // 3. GST BIFURCATION MATH
    let igst = 0, cgst = 0, sgst = 0;
    if (vendorState === "Andhra Pradesh" || vendorState.includes("37")) { 
        cgst = (gstVal / 2).toFixed(2); 
        sgst = (gstVal / 2).toFixed(2); 
    } else { 
        igst = gstVal.toFixed(2); 
    }

    // 4. BUILD PAYLOAD
    const d = {
        poNum: document.getElementById('po-no').value, 
        date: document.getElementById('po-date').value, 
        status: document.getElementById('po-status').value,
        vendor: document.getElementById('po-vendor').value, 
        vendorAddr: document.getElementById('po-vendor-addr').value,
        site: document.getElementById('po-site').value, 
        shipAddr: document.getElementById('po-ship-addr').value,
        subject: document.getElementById('po-subject').value, 
        taxRate: document.getElementById('po-gstrate').value,
        subtotal: document.getElementById('po-subtotal').innerText.replace(/[^\d.-]/g, ''),
        
        // --- NEW: SENDING BIFURCATED TAXES TO BACKEND PDF GENERATOR ---
        gstAmt: gstVal, // Keep total for the database row
        igst: igst,     
        cgst: cgst,     
        sgst: sgst,     
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        
        total: document.getElementById('po-total').innerText.replace(/[^\d.-]/g, ''),
        notes: document.getElementById('po-notes').value, 
        terms: document.getElementById('po-terms').value, 
        lastModified: document.getElementById('po-lastModified').value,
        items: JSON.stringify([...document.querySelectorAll('#po-items tr')].map(r => ({
            desc: r.querySelector('.d').value, 
            costHead: r.querySelector('.c').value, 
            sac: r.querySelector('.s').value, 
            uom: r.querySelector('.u').value, 
            qty: r.querySelector('.q').value, 
            rate: r.querySelector('.r').value, 
            amount: r.querySelector('.a').value
        })))
    };

    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
        hideLoad(); 
        const r = JSON.parse(res);
        if(r.status === 'success') {
            Swal.fire('Saved', 'Purchase Order Generated!', 'success');
            document.getElementById('btn-save-po').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('po-actions').style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('po-pdf-link').href = r.url;
            silentSync();
        } else {
            Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
        }
    }).savePO(token, JSON.stringify(d));
}
    function render() {
        let currentPage = 1;
        const ROWS_PER_PAGE = 10;
        const d = new Date();
        const y = d.getFullYear();
        const m = d.getMonth() + 1;
        const fy = m >= 4 ? `${String(y).slice(-2)}-${String(y+1).slice(-2)}` : `${String(y-1).slice(-2)}-${String(y).slice(-2)}`;
        const fyEl = document.getElementById('top-fy-badge');
        if(fyEl) fyEl.innerText = "FY " + fy;
        if(!DATA || !DATA.dashboard) return;
        document.getElementById('d-sales').innerText = "₹" + (DATA.dashboard.sales || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');
        document.getElementById('d-rec').innerText = "₹" + (DATA.dashboard.received || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');
        document.getElementById('d-due').innerText = "₹" + (DATA.dashboard.due || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');
        document.getElementById('d-count').innerText = DATA.dashboard.count || 0;

        if(DATA.dashboard.breakdown) {
           try {
             const t = document.getElementById('card-inv-total');
             t.setAttribute('title', `Pending: ${DATA.dashboard.breakdown.pending} | Received: ${DATA.dashboard.breakdown.received}`);
             new bootstrap.Tooltip(t);
           } catch(e){}
        }
       
        let clientOpts = '<option value="">Select Client...</option>';
        if(DATA.clients) { DATA.clients.forEach(c => { clientOpts += `<option value="${c.idx}">${c.name} - ${c.site}</option>`; }); }
        document.getElementById('pay-client').innerHTML = clientOpts;
        // 🚀 NEW: Populate WO Dropdown in Payments
    const payWoSelect = document.getElementById('pay-wo');
    if (payWoSelect && DATA.workOrders) {
        let currentWo = payWoSelect.value;
        payWoSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">No Link</option>';
        DATA.workOrders.forEach(w => payWoSelect.add(new Option(`${w.woNo} (${w.client})`, w.woNo)));
        payWoSelect.value = currentWo;
    }
        
        const bpSelect = document.getElementById('bpClient');
        if(bpSelect && DATA.clients) { bpSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select...</option>' + DATA.clients.map(c => `<option value="${c.name}">${c.name} (${c.site})</option>`).join(''); }
        
        const ledClient = document.getElementById('led-client');
        ledClient.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select...</option>';
        if(DATA.clients) { const uniqueCompanies = [...new Set(DATA.clients.map(function(c) { return c.name; }))];
        uniqueCompanies.forEach(function(comp) { let opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = comp; opt.text = comp; ledClient.add(opt); }); }
        
        const pnlSite = document.getElementById('pnl-site-select');
        const paySite = document.getElementById('pay-site');
        const chFilterSite = document.getElementById('ch-filter-site');
        const chAddSite = document.getElementById('add-ch-site');
        const uniqueSites = [...new Set((DATA.clients||[]).map(c => c.site || "Main Site"))].sort();
        
        pnlSite.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Site...</option>'; paySite.innerHTML = '<option value="">General Expense</option>';
        if(chFilterSite) chFilterSite.innerHTML = '<option value="All Sites">All Sites</option>';
        if(chAddSite) chAddSite.innerHTML = '<option value="All">All Sites</option>';
        uniqueSites.forEach(s => { 
            pnlSite.add(new Option(s, s)); paySite.add(new Option(s, s)); 
            if(chFilterSite) chFilterSite.add(new Option(s, s)); if(chAddSite) chAddSite.add(new Option(s, s));
        });

        if(DATA.clients) {
            const totalClientPages = Math.ceil(DATA.clients.length / CLIENTS_PER_PAGE) || 1;
            if (currentClientPage > totalClientPages) currentClientPage = totalClientPages;
            const startCIdx = (currentClientPage - 1) * CLIENTS_PER_PAGE;
            const paginatedClients = DATA.clients.slice(startCIdx, startCIdx + CLIENTS_PER_PAGE);

            document.getElementById('tbl-clients').innerHTML = paginatedClients.map(function(c) { 
                const i = DATA.clients.indexOf(c); // Keeps the exact index for edit/delete functions
                const delBtn = userRole === 'admin' ? `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="del('Clients','${c.id}')"><i class="bi bi-trash-fill"></i></button>` : '';
                return '<tr><td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">' + c.name + '</span></td><td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border rounded-pill">' + (c.site || "-") + '</span></td><td><small class="font-monospace text-muted">' + c.gstin + '</small></td><td>' + c.state + '</td><td class="text-end"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary shadow-sm" onclick="editClient(' + i + ')"><i class="bi bi-pencil-fill"></i></button><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-success ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="copyClient(' + i + ')" title="Duplicate"><i class="bi bi-files"></i></button>' + delBtn + '</td></tr>'; 
            }).join('');

            let clientPagination = document.getElementById('client-pagination-controls');
            if(!clientPagination) {
                clientPagination = document.createElement('div');
                clientPagination.id = 'client-pagination-controls';
                document.getElementById('tbl-clients-main').closest('.card').appendChild(clientPagination);
            }
            clientPagination.innerHTML = `
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mt-3 mb-2 px-3 border-top pt-3">
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changeClientPage(-1)" ${currentClientPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}><i class="bi bi-chevron-left"></i> Previous</button>
                    <span class="text-muted small fw-bold">Page ${currentClientPage} of ${totalClientPages}</span>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changeClientPage(1)" ${currentClientPage === totalClientPages ? 'disabled' : ''}>Next <i class="bi bi-chevron-right"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        document.getElementById('tbl-vendors').innerHTML = (DATA.vendors||[]).map(function(v, i) { 
            const delBtn = userRole === 'admin' ? `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="del('Vendors','${v.id}')"><i class="bi bi-trash-fill"></i></button>` : '';
            return '<tr><td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">' + v.name + '</span><br><small class="text-muted">' + v.id + '</small></td><td>' + v.phone + '</td><td>' + v.email + '</td><td>' + v.gstin + '</td><td class="text-end"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary shadow-sm" onclick="editVendor(' + i + ')"><i class="bi bi-pencil-fill"></i></button>' + delBtn + '</td></tr>'; 
        }).join('');

        // --- PAGINATION VARIABLES (Declare these OUTSIDE the render function, near the top of your scripts) ---
        // let currentPage = 1;
        // const ROWS_PER_PAGE = 50;

        if(DATA.invoices) {
            // 1. Calculate Pagination
            const totalPages = Math.ceil(DATA.invoices.length / INV_PER_PAGE) || 1;
            if (currentInvPage > totalPages) currentInvPage = totalPages;
            const startIdx = (currentInvPage - 1) * INV_PER_PAGE;
            const paginatedData = DATA.invoices.slice(startIdx, startIdx + INV_PER_PAGE);

            // 2. Draw only 50 rows!
            document.getElementById('tbl-invoices').innerHTML = paginatedData.map(function(i) {
                const idx = DATA.invoices.indexOf(i); // Crucial for Edit/Pay buttons to work
                
                let b = 'bg-secondary';
                if (i.status === 'Received') b = 'bg-success';
                else if (i.status === 'Partial') b = 'bg-warning text-dark';
                else if (i.status === 'Pending') b = 'bg-danger';
                else if (i.status === 'Cancelled') b = 'bg-dark';
                
                let statusBadge = i.status ? `<span class="badge ${b} rounded-pill status-badge px-3 shadow-sm">${i.status}</span>` : "";
                
                let gstBadge = "";
                let gstActionText = "Mark GST Paid";
                if(i.gstStatus === "GST_PAID") {
                     gstActionText = "Unmark GST Paid";
                     gstBadge = `<span class="badge bg-info text-dark ms-1 shadow-sm rounded-pill" style="font-size:0.65em"><i class="bi bi-shield-check me-1"></i>PAID</span>`;
                }

                const d = Math.floor((new Date() - new Date(i.date)) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                let ageClass = "";
                let ageDisplay = d + " Days";
                if (i.status === 'Received') {
                    ageDisplay = "Paid";
                    ageClass = "text-success fw-bold";
                } else {
                    if(d > 90) ageClass = "text-danger fw-bold";
                    else if(d > 30) ageClass = "text-warning fw-bold";
                }

                let actionBtn = '';
                if(userRole === 'admin') {
                    if (i.status === 'Cancelled') { 
                        actionBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-success fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="restoreInv('${i.invNum}')"><i class="bi bi-arrow-counterclockwise me-2"></i>Restore Invoice</a></li>`;
                    } else {
                        actionBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-danger fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="cancelInv('${i.invNum}')"><i class="bi bi-x-circle me-2"></i>Cancel Invoice</a></li>`;
                    }
                }
                
                // --- START OF ACTION BUTTON LOGIC ---
                let convertBtn = "";
                let payBtn = "";
                let currentStatus = (i.status || "").toUpperCase().trim();
                
                // 1. Status Block Logic
                const isFinalized = currentStatus === "RECEIVED" || currentStatus === "PAID" || currentStatus === "CANCELLED";
                const isConverted = currentStatus.includes("CONVERTED");

                // 2. Conversion Buttons (Proforma, Quotes, DC)
                if((i.type === "Quotation" || i.type === "Delivery Challan" || i.type === "Proforma Invoice") && !isFinalized && !isConverted) {
                    convertBtn += `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-primary fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="convertQuote(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-receipt me-2"></i>Convert to Tax Invoice</a></li>`;
                    if (i.type !== "Delivery Challan") {
                        convertBtn += `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-warning fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="convertToDC(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-truck me-2"></i>Convert to DC</a></li>`;
                    }
                }

                // 3. Mathematical & Status Payment Lock
                let remainingDue = parseFloat(i.due) || 0;
                let isMathematicallyPaid = remainingDue <= 0 || currentStatus.includes("CANCELLED");
                
                // 🚀 FIXED: We ONLY lock the button if the invoice was Converted. 
                // Normal Drafts are now fully unlocked so you can receive Advances!
                const isCancelConverted = (i.cancelStatus || "").toUpperCase().trim().includes("CONVERTED");
                const isLockedStatus = isConverted || isCancelConverted; 

                if(i.type === "Tax Invoice" || i.type === "Proforma Invoice") {
                    if (isLockedStatus) {
                        // If it's a Converted PI, hide the button completely!
                        payBtn = ""; 
                    } else if (isMathematicallyPaid) {
                        // Disabled version if mathematically fully paid or cancelled
                        payBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-muted" href="javascript:void(0)" style="cursor: not-allowed; opacity: 0.6;"><i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill text-success me-2"></i>Payment Received</a></li>`;
                    } else {
                        // Active version allowing multiple partial payments until Due hits 0
                        payBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="openPay(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-wallet2 text-success me-2"></i>Record Payment</a></li>`;
                    }
                }
                // --- END OF ACTION BUTTON LOGIC ---
                
                let gstBtn = "";
                let tdsBtn = "";
                if(i.type === "Tax Invoice") {
                     gstBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="toggleGST('${i.invNum}')"><i class="bi bi-shield-check text-info me-2"></i>${gstActionText}</a></li>`;
                     if(i.status !== "Cancelled" && userRole === 'admin') {
                         tdsBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item text-warning fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="promptTDS('${i.invNum}')"><i class="bi bi-scissors me-2"></i>Record TDS</a></li>`;
                     }
                }

                let emailBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="quickEmailInv(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-envelope text-primary me-2"></i>Email Client</a></li>`;
                let waBtn = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="quickWaInv(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-whatsapp text-success me-2"></i>WhatsApp Client</a></li>`;
                const delBtnHtml = userRole === 'admin' ? `<li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li><li><a class="dropdown-item text-danger fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="del('Invoices','${i.invNum}')"><i class="bi bi-trash me-2"></i>Delete permanently</a></li>` : '';
                
                const menu = `<div class="dropdown"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border shadow-sm px-2" type="button" data-bs-toggle="dropdown"><i class="bi bi-three-dots-vertical"></i></button><ul class="dropdown-menu shadow-lg border-0 p-2" style="border-radius:16px;">${payBtn}${waBtn}${emailBtn}<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="duplicateInvoice(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-files text-secondary me-2"></i>Duplicate</a></li>${convertBtn}<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="editInvoice(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-pencil text-primary me-2"></i>Edit</a></li>${gstBtn}${tdsBtn}<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="${i.pdf}" target="_blank"><i class="bi bi-file-pdf text-danger me-2"></i>View PDF</a></li>${actionBtn}${delBtnHtml}</ul></div>`;
                
                const woBadge = i.soNumber ? `<br><span class="text-secondary fw-bold" style="font-size:0.75em"><i class="bi bi-link-45deg"></i>${i.soNumber}</span>` : "";
                
                const safeCompany = escapeHtml(i.company);
                const safeSite = escapeHtml(i.siteName);

                // Replace your existing invoice return line with this one:
                return `<tr data-type="${i.type}">
                    <td data-label="Invoice Details"><span class="text-primary fw-bold font-monospace small">${i.invNum}</span>${gstBadge}<br><small class="text-muted" style="font-size:0.75em">${i.type}</small>${woBadge}</td>
                    <td data-label="Date">${i.date}</td>
                    <td data-label="Client & Site"><span class="fw-bold text-dark">${safeCompany}</span><br><small class="text-muted">${safeSite}</small></td>
                    <td data-label="Amount" class="fw-bold fs-6">₹${(i.total || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td data-label="Status">${statusBadge}</td>
                    <td data-label="Age"><small class="${ageClass}">${ageDisplay}</small></td>
                    <td data-label="Actions" class="text-end">${menu}</td>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');

            // 3. Draw Pagination Controls
            let paginationControls = document.getElementById('pagination-controls');
            if(!paginationControls) {
                paginationControls = document.createElement('div');
                paginationControls.id = 'pagination-controls';
                document.getElementById('inv-list-container').appendChild(paginationControls);
            }
            
            paginationControls.innerHTML = `
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mt-3 mb-2 px-3 border-top pt-3">
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changePage(-1)" ${currentInvPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}><i class="bi bi-chevron-left"></i> Previous</button>
                    <span class="text-muted small fw-bold">Page ${currentInvPage} of ${totalPages}</span>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changePage(1)" ${currentInvPage === totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''}>Next <i class="bi bi-chevron-right"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        if(DATA.purchases) {
             const vList = document.getElementById('list-vendors');
             vList.innerHTML = "";
             if(DATA.vendors) DATA.vendors.forEach(v => vList.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<option value="${v.name}">`));
             
             document.getElementById('tbl-purchases').innerHTML = DATA.purchases.map((p, idx) => {
                 const delBtn = userRole === 'admin' ? `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="del('Purchases','${p.id}')"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button>` : '';
                 return `<tr><td>${p.date}</td><td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">${p.vendor}</span></td><td><small class="font-monospace">${p.gstin || '-'}</small></td><td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border rounded-pill">${p.invNo}</span></td><td>${(p.taxable||0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td><td>${(p.tax||0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td><td class="fw-bold text-danger">₹${(p.total||0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td><td class="text-muted small">${p.desc}</td><td class="text-end"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary shadow-sm" onclick="editPurchase(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-pencil-fill"></i></button>${delBtn}</td></tr>`;
             }).join('');
        }
       // --- RENDER WORK ORDERS ---
        if(DATA.workOrders) {
            document.getElementById('tbl-workorders').innerHTML = DATA.workOrders.map(function(w) {
                let statusBadge = 'bg-secondary';
                let rawStatus = String(w.status).trim();

                // SMART INVOICE MATCHER (SUPPORTS MULTIPLE INVOICES)
                let linkedInvHtml = '-';
                const matchingInvs = (DATA.invoices || []).filter(i => String(i.soNumber).trim().toUpperCase() === String(w.woNo).trim().toUpperCase() && i.status !== 'Cancelled');

                if (matchingInvs.length > 0) {
                    // Creates a row of multiple blue badges if there are multiple invoices!
                    linkedInvHtml = matchingInvs.map(inv => 
                        `<a href="${inv.pdf}" target="_blank" class="badge bg-primary text-decoration-none shadow-sm py-1 px-2 mb-1 fs-7 me-1 d-inline-block"><i class="bi bi-file-pdf me-1"></i> ${inv.invNum}</a>`
                    ).join('');
                } else if (w.invLink && w.invLink.startsWith('http')) {
                    linkedInvHtml = `<a href="${w.invLink}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary py-0 rounded-pill shadow-sm"><i class="bi bi-link-45deg"></i> View</a>`;
                }

                // --- SMART FINANCIAL MATH (Total, Billed, Balance) ---
                let woBaseTotal = 0;
                try {
                    let wItems = JSON.parse(w.items || "[]");
                    woBaseTotal = wItems.reduce((sum, it) => sum + (parseFloat(it.qty||0) * parseFloat(it.rate||0)), 0);
                } catch(e){}

                let billedTotal = 0;
                matchingInvs.forEach(inv => {
                    try {
                        let invItems = JSON.parse(inv.items || "[]");
                        billedTotal += invItems.reduce((sum, it) => sum + parseFloat(it.amount||0), 0);
                    } catch(e){}
                });

                let balanceTotal = woBaseTotal - billedTotal;
                if (balanceTotal < 0) balanceTotal = 0; // Prevent negative balance if overbilled

                // --- DYNAMIC STATUS OVERRIDE ---
                // The frontend will automatically force the status based on the real math!
                if (billedTotal > 0 && balanceTotal > 0.5) {
                    rawStatus = 'Partial';
                } else if (billedTotal > 0 && balanceTotal <= 0.5) {
                    rawStatus = 'Completed';
                }

                // Determine Badge Color
                if(rawStatus === 'Completed') statusBadge = 'bg-success';
                else if(rawStatus === 'In Progress') statusBadge = 'bg-warning text-dark';
                else if(rawStatus === 'Partial') statusBadge = 'bg-info text-dark';
                else if(rawStatus === 'Pending') statusBadge = 'bg-danger';
                
                // Build the new Financials Column
                let finHtml = `<small>
                    <b>Total:</b> ₹${woBaseTotal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}<br>
                    <span class="text-success"><b>Billed:</b> ₹${billedTotal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</span><br>
                    <span class="${balanceTotal > 0 ? 'text-danger' : 'text-muted'}"><b>Bal:</b> ₹${balanceTotal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</span>
                </small>`;

                // 3. Assemble the Menu
                let convertBtnHtml = '';
                if (rawStatus !== 'Completed') {
                    convertBtnHtml = `<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="convertWOtoInvoice('${w.woNo}')"><i class="bi bi-receipt text-success me-2"></i>Convert to Invoice</a></li>`;
                }
                const delBtnHtml = userRole === 'admin' ? `<li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li><li><a class="dropdown-item text-danger fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="del('WorkOrders','${w.woNo}')"><i class="bi bi-trash me-2"></i>Delete</a></li>` : '';
                const menu = `<div class="dropdown"><button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border shadow-sm px-2" type="button" data-bs-toggle="dropdown"><i class="bi bi-three-dots-vertical"></i></button><ul class="dropdown-menu shadow-lg border-0 p-2" style="border-radius:16px;">${convertBtnHtml}<li><a class="dropdown-item fw-bold" href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="editWO('${w.woNo}')"><i class="bi bi-pencil text-primary me-2"></i>Edit Work Order</a></li>${delBtnHtml}</ul></div>`;

                return `<tr>
                    <td><a href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="editWO('${w.woNo}')" class="fw-bold text-primary font-monospace text-decoration-none" title="Edit WO">${w.woNo} <i class="bi bi-pencil-square ms-1"></i></a></td>
                    <td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">${w.client}</span><br><small class="text-muted">${w.site}</small></td>
                    <td>${w.desc}</td>
                    <td><i class="bi bi-person-circle me-1 text-muted"></i> ${w.staff}</td>
                    <td>${finHtml}</td>
                    <td><span class="badge ${statusBadge} rounded-pill shadow-sm px-3">${rawStatus}</span></td>
                    <td class="text-center">${linkedInvHtml}</td>
                    <td class="text-end">${menu}</td>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
        }

        if(DATA.payments) {
            const totalPayPages = Math.ceil(DATA.payments.length / PAY_PER_PAGE) || 1;
            if (currentPayPage > totalPayPages) currentPayPage = totalPayPages;
            const startPIdx = (currentPayPage - 1) * PAY_PER_PAGE;
            const paginatedPays = DATA.payments.slice(startPIdx, startPIdx + PAY_PER_PAGE);

            document.getElementById('tbl-payments').innerHTML = paginatedPays.map(function(p) {
                const idx = DATA.payments.indexOf(p); // Need exact idx for Edit button
                const date = p.date || "-"; const client = p.client || "-"; const amount = p.amount ? "₹" + parseFloat(Math.abs(p.amount)).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) : "₹0";
                const ref = p.ref || "-"; const rowClass = p.type === "GST Payment" ? "table-warning" : "";
                const rcpt = p.receiptUrl ? `<a href="${p.receiptUrl}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary py-0 rounded-pill shadow-sm"><i class="bi bi-link-45deg"></i> View</a>` : '-';
                
                let amtDisplay = `<span class="fw-bold text-success">${amount}</span>`;
                if(p.type === 'Vendor Payment' || p.type === 'Expense' || p.amount < 0) {
                    amtDisplay = `<span class="fw-bold text-danger">- ${amount}</span>`;
                }

                let delBtn = `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="editPayment(${idx})"><i class="bi bi-pencil-fill"></i></button>`;
                if(userRole === 'admin') {
                    delBtn += `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-danger ms-1 shadow-sm" onclick="del('Payments','${p.id}')"><i class="bi bi-trash-fill"></i></button>`;
                }

                return `<tr class="${rowClass}"><td>${date}</td><td><span class="fw-bold text-dark">${client}</span><br><small class="text-muted">${p.type}</small></td><td>${amtDisplay}</td><td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border rounded-pill">${ref}</span></td><td class="text-center">${rcpt}</td><td class="text-end">${delBtn}</td></tr>`;
            }).join('');

            let payPagination = document.getElementById('pay-pagination-controls');
            if(!payPagination) {
                payPagination = document.createElement('div');
                payPagination.id = 'pay-pagination-controls';
                document.getElementById('hist-area').appendChild(payPagination);
            }
            payPagination.innerHTML = `
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mt-3 mb-2 px-3 border-top pt-3">
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changePayPage(-1)" ${currentPayPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}><i class="bi bi-chevron-left"></i> Previous</button>
                    <span class="text-muted small fw-bold">Page ${currentPayPage} of ${totalPayPages}</span>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary shadow-sm" onclick="changePayPage(1)" ${currentPayPage === totalPayPages ? 'disabled' : ''}>Next <i class="bi bi-chevron-right"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        // 🚀 NEW: Populate WO Dropdown in Purchases
    const purWoSelect = document.getElementById('pur-wo');
    if (purWoSelect && DATA.workOrders) {
        let currentWo = purWoSelect.value;
        purWoSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">No Link</option>';
        DATA.workOrders.forEach(w => purWoSelect.add(new Option(`${w.woNo} (${w.client})`, w.woNo)));
        purWoSelect.value = currentWo;
    }
        
        if (DATA.gstTracker) {
            const trBody = document.getElementById('tbl-gst-tracker-body');
            if (trBody) {
                trBody.innerHTML = DATA.gstTracker.map(t => {
                    const status1 = t.gstr1 === "Filed" ? "bg-success" : "bg-warning text-dark";
                    const status3 = t.gstr3b === "Filed" ? "bg-success" : "bg-warning text-dark";
                    return `<tr><td class="fw-bold">${t.month}</td><td><span class="badge ${status1} rounded-pill shadow-sm">${t.gstr1}</span></td><td><span class="badge ${status3} rounded-pill shadow-sm">${t.gstr3b}</span></td><td class="text-success fw-bold">₹${parseFloat(t.paid||0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td><td><span class="font-monospace text-muted">${t.challan}</span></td><td>${t.date}</td></tr>`;
                }).join('');
            }
        }
        
        if(typeof renderSalesReport === "function") renderSalesReport();
        applyRBAC();
        // Render Purchase Orders Table
        if (typeof renderPOs === 'function') renderPOs();
    }
    
    // --- TEMPLATE DATA INCLUDED HERE ---
    function saveInvoice(e) { 
        e.preventDefault();
        
        // 1. Pre-Flight Validation
        let missingCostHead = false;
        document.querySelectorAll('#i-items tr').forEach(function(r) { 
            if(!r.querySelector('.c').value.trim()) missingCostHead = true;
        });
        if(missingCostHead) {
            return Swal.fire({ icon: 'warning', title: 'Cost Head Required', text: 'Please select a Cost Head for every item line before saving.' });
        }

        showLoad();
        
        try { 
            if(!document.getElementById('i-company').value) throw new Error("Please select a company");
            if(!document.getElementById('i-site-select').value) throw new Error("Please select a site");
            
            const invDate = new Date(document.getElementById('i-date').value); 
            const today = new Date();
            const billToDate = document.getElementById('i-to').value ? new Date(document.getElementById('i-to').value) : null;
            
            if (invDate > today) throw new Error("Invoice Date cannot be in the future.");
            if (billToDate && billToDate > invDate) throw new Error("Bill Period 'To' cannot be after Invoice Date."); 
            
            const siteText = document.getElementById('i-site-select').options[document.getElementById('i-site-select').selectedIndex].text;
            const state = document.getElementById('i-state').value; 
            
            // Strip commas safely
            const gstVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('i-gst').innerText.replace(/,/g, '')) || 0; 
            let igst = 0, cgst = 0, sgst = 0;
            if (state === "Andhra Pradesh") { 
                cgst = (gstVal / 2).toFixed(2); 
                sgst = (gstVal / 2).toFixed(2); 
            } else { 
                igst = gstVal.toFixed(2); 
            } 
            
            // 2. Build Payload
            const d = { 
                invNum: document.getElementById('i-no').value, 
                invType: document.getElementById('i-type').value, 
                revNo: document.getElementById('i-rev') ? document.getElementById('i-rev').value : "", 
                date: document.getElementById('i-date').value, 
                clientName: document.getElementById('i-company').value, 
                siteName: siteText, 
                gstin: document.getElementById('i-gstin').value, 
                clientState: document.getElementById('i-state').value, 
                billFrom: document.getElementById('i-from').value, 
                billTo: document.getElementById('i-to').value, 
                billingAddress: document.getElementById('i-addr').value, 
                shipping: document.getElementById('i-ship').value, 
                vehicle: document.getElementById('i-veh').value, 
                soNumber: document.getElementById('i-so').value, 
                gstRate: document.getElementById('i-gstrate').value, 
                subTotal: document.getElementById('i-item-total').innerText.replace(/,/g, ''), 
                igst: igst, cgst: cgst, sgst: sgst, 
                grandTotal: document.getElementById('i-total').innerText.replace(/,/g, ''), 
                words: document.getElementById('i-words').value, 
                workDesc: document.getElementById('i-desc').value, 
                terms: document.getElementById('i-terms').value, 
                roundOff: document.getElementById('i-round').innerText.replace(/,/g, ''), 
                templateId: document.getElementById('i-template') ? document.getElementById('i-template').value : "1",
                itemsJson: JSON.stringify([...document.querySelectorAll('#i-items tr')].map(function(r) { 
                    return { 
                        desc: r.querySelector('.d').value, 
                        costHead: r.querySelector('.c').value, 
                        sac: r.querySelector('.s').value, 
                        uom: r.querySelector('.u').value, 
                        qty: r.querySelector('.q').value, 
                        rate: r.querySelector('.r').value, 
                        amount: r.querySelector('.a').value.replace(/,/g, '') 
                    };
                })), 
                lastModified: document.getElementById('i-lastModified').value,
                
                // 🚀 NEW: Grab the secret stamp so the backend can Auto-Link payments!
                convertedFrom: document.getElementById('convertedFrom') ? document.getElementById('convertedFrom').value : ""
            };

            // 3. Fetch Token
            const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');

            // 4. Server Execution with Crash Nets
            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
                    let r;
                    try {
                        r = (typeof res === 'string') ? JSON.parse(res) : res;
                    } catch(parseErr) {
                        hideLoad();
                        return Swal.fire('Server Error', 'Received garbled data from the server.', 'error');
                    }

                    if (r.status === "success") { 
                        document.getElementById('i-pdf-link').href = r.url; 
                        document.getElementById('inv-actions').style.display = 'flex'; 
                        document.getElementById('btn-save-inv').style.display = 'none'; 
                        
                        // 🚀 Call silentSync FIRST. Put everything else inside the callback!
                        silentSync(() => {
                            hideLoad(); // Hide spinner AFTER data arrives
                            Swal.fire({ title: 'Success', text: 'Saved! QR Code applied.', icon: 'success', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });
                        });
                    } else { 
                        hideLoad();
                        Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error'); 
                    } 
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) { 
                    hideLoad(); 
                    // Safely extract the error message from the Google Apps Script Error Object
                    const errorText = err.message ? err.message : err;
                    console.error("Backend Error:", errorText);
                    Swal.fire('Server Error', errorText, 'error'); 
                })
                .saveInvoice(token, JSON.stringify(d)); // 👈 Token MUST be parameter 1

        } catch (error) { 
            hideLoad(); 
            Swal.fire('Validation Error', error.message, 'warning'); 
        } 
    }
    // Include the remaining essential helper functions verbatim as requested
    function calc() { 
        let t = 0;
        document.querySelectorAll('#i-items tr').forEach(function(r) { 
            const q = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.q').value) || 0; const rt = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.r').value) || 0; 
            const a = (q * rt).toFixed(2); r.querySelector('.a').value = a; t += parseFloat(a); 
        });
        const r = parseFloat(document.getElementById('i-gstrate').value); const g = t * (r / 100); const e = t + g;
        const rd = Math.round(e); const off = rd - e; 
        document.getElementById('i-item-total').innerText = t.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}); document.getElementById('i-gst').innerText = g.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
        document.getElementById('i-round').innerText = off.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}); document.getElementById('i-total').innerText = rd.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}); 
        document.getElementById('i-words').value = numToWords(rd) + " Only";
    }

    // I have retained the remaining ~30+ front-end JS functions (loadGSTR3B, editPurchase, filterInvoices, etc.) exactly as they were in your original code to avoid truncating this box. They exist inside your file. 
    // Just ensure the functions above overwrite your current saveInvoice and render blocks.
    function getBase64(file) {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result.split(',')[1]);
            reader.onerror = error => reject(error);
        });
    }

    function calcPur() {
        const t = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pur-taxable').value) || 0;
        const x = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pur-tax').value) || 0;
        document.getElementById('pur-total').value = (t + x).toFixed(2);
    }
    
    function autoFillVendorGST() {
        const vName = document.getElementById('pur-vendor').value;
        const vObj = DATA.vendors.find(v => v.name === vName);
        if(vObj) document.getElementById('pur-gstin').value = vObj.gstin || "";
    }
    
    function savePurchase(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const taxable = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pur-taxable').value)||0;
        const tax = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pur-tax').value)||0;
        const est28 = taxable * 0.28;
        const est18 = taxable * 0.18;
        const est12 = taxable * 0.12;
        const est5 = taxable * 0.05;
        const est3 = taxable * 0.03;
        
        if(tax > 0 && Math.abs(tax - est28) > 2 && Math.abs(tax - est18) > 2 && Math.abs(tax - est12) > 2 && Math.abs(tax - est5) > 2 && Math.abs(tax - est3) > 2) {
            if(!confirm("Warning: Tax amount seems inconsistent with standard GST rates (3%, 5%, 12%, 18%, 28%). Continue?")) return;
        }

        showLoad();
        // Update the 'const d' payload to include woNo
    const d = { 
        id: document.getElementById('pur-id').value, 
        date: document.getElementById('pur-date').value,
