        vendor: document.getElementById('pur-vendor').value, 
        gstin: document.getElementById('pur-gstin').value, 
        invNo: document.getElementById('pur-inv').value, 
        taxable: taxable, tax: tax, total: document.getElementById('pur-total').value, 
        desc: document.getElementById('pur-desc').value, 
        costHead: document.getElementById('pur-costhead').value, 
        woNo: document.getElementById('pur-wo') ? document.getElementById('pur-wo').value : "", // 🚀 NEW 
        lastModified: document.getElementById('pur-lastModified').value 
    };
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler((r) => {
            const res = JSON.parse(r);
            if (res.status === "error") {
                hideLoad();
                return Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
            }
            
            // 🚀 Call silentSync FIRST. Put everything else inside the callback!
            silentSync(() => {
                hideLoad(); // Hide spinner AFTER data arrives
                Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: 'Purchase Saved'}); 
                document.getElementById('pur-id').value = ""; 
                document.getElementById('pur-lastModified').value = "";
                e.target.reset();
            });
        }).savePurchase(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), JSON.stringify(d));
    }
    
    function editPurchase(i) {
        const p = DATA.purchases[i];
        
        // FIX: Force the collapsible purchase form to open so it becomes visible!
        document.getElementById('collapsePurchase').classList.add('show');
        document.getElementById('pur-id').value = p.id;
        document.getElementById('pur-lastModified').value = p.lastModified || "";
        document.getElementById('pur-date').value = p.date;
        document.getElementById('pur-vendor').value = p.vendor;
        document.getElementById('pur-gstin').value = p.gstin;
        document.getElementById('pur-inv').value = p.invNo;
        document.getElementById('pur-taxable').value = p.taxable;
        document.getElementById('pur-tax').value = p.tax;
        document.getElementById('pur-total').value = p.total;
        document.getElementById('pur-desc').value = p.desc;
        document.getElementById('pur-costhead').value = p.costHead || "";
        if(document.getElementById('pur-wo')) document.getElementById('pur-wo').value = p.woNo || ""; // 🚀 NEW
        document.getElementById('pur-lastModified').value = p.lastModified || "";
        
        // Scroll smoothly to the top of the card where the form is
        document.querySelector('#view-purchases .card').scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth'});
    }
    
   function toggleGST(invNum) {
    Swal.fire({title: 'Confirm', text: "Toggle GST Paid status?", icon: 'question', showCancelButton: true}).then((result) => {
      if (result.isConfirmed) {
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
            hideLoad();
            const response = JSON.parse(res);
            if(response.status === "error") {
                Swal.fire('Error', response.message, 'error');
            } else {
                Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: 'Updated'});
                loadData();
            }
        }).toggleInvoiceGST(invNum);
      }
    });
   }

    function promptTDS(invNum) {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Record TDS Deduction',
            html: `<p class="text-muted">Enter the exact TDS amount deducted by the client against Invoice <b>${invNum}</b>.</p>
                   <input type="number" id="tds-amt" class="swal2-input" placeholder="TDS Amount (₹)">`,
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Post TDS',
            preConfirm: () => {
                const amt = document.getElementById('tds-amt').value;
                if(!amt || amt <= 0) Swal.showValidationMessage('Enter a valid amount');
                return amt;
            }
        }).then(res => {
            if(res.isConfirmed) {
                showLoad();
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(serverRes => {
                    hideLoad();
                    const parsed = JSON.parse(serverRes);
                    if(parsed.status === 'success') { Swal.fire('Success', parsed.message, 'success'); loadData(); }
                    else Swal.fire('Error', parsed.message, 'error');
                }).recordTDS(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), JSON.stringify({invNum: invNum, amount: res.value}));
            }
        });
    }
    
    function doBackup() {
        Swal.fire({title: 'Backup', text: "Create a full backup in Drive?", icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true}).then((result) => {
            if(result.isConfirmed) {
                showLoad();
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
                    hideLoad();
                    Swal.fire('Success', JSON.parse(r).message, 'success');
                }).createBackup();
            }
        });
    }

    // ==========================================
    // GSTR-2A MANUAL ENTRY UI
    // ==========================================
    function promptGSTR2A() {
        Swal.fire({
            title: '<i class="bi bi-file-earmark-spreadsheet text-success fs-1"></i><br>Bulk Upload B2B CSV',
            html: `
                <div class="text-start small">
                    <p class="text-muted">Upload the raw <b>B2B.csv</b> file downloaded directly from the GST Portal.</p>
                    <label class="form-label fw-bold">Target Month (YYYY-MM) *</label>
                    <input id="bulk-month" type="month" class="form-control mb-3">
                    <label class="form-label fw-bold">Select CSV File *</label>
                    <input id="bulk-csv" type="file" accept=".csv" class="form-control">
                </div>
            `,
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Upload & Parse',
            confirmButtonColor: '#1a73e8',
            preConfirm: () => {
                const month = document.getElementById('bulk-month').value;
                const file = document.getElementById('bulk-csv').files[0];
                if (!month) { Swal.showValidationMessage("Please select the Target Month."); return false; }
                if (!file) { Swal.showValidationMessage("Please select a CSV file."); return false; }
                return { month, file };
            }
        }).then((res) => {
            if (res.isConfirmed) {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(e) {
                    const text = e.target.result;
                    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
                    let records = [];
                    
                    for(let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
                        if(!lines[i].trim()) continue;
                        
                        // Regex to handle commas inside quotes
                        let row = lines[i].split(/,(?=(?:(?:[^"]*"){2})*[^"]*$)/);
                        
                        if(row.length > 10 && row[0].trim()) {
                            let gstin = row[0].replace(/"/g, '').trim().toUpperCase();
                            let invNo = row[2].replace(/"/g, '').trim();
                            
                            // 🚨 STRICT AI FILTER: 
                            // 1. Must be exactly 15 characters (Valid GSTIN)
                            // 2. MUST NOT contain the word "-Total"
                            if(gstin.length === 15 && !invNo.toUpperCase().includes("-TOTAL")) {
                                
                                let name = row[1].replace(/"/g, '').trim();
                                let date = row[4].replace(/"/g, '').trim();
                                
                                let taxable = parseFloat(row[9].replace(/["',]/g, '')) || 0;
                                let igst = parseFloat(row[10].replace(/["',]/g, '')) || 0;
                                let cgst = parseFloat(row[11].replace(/["',]/g, '')) || 0;
                                let sgst = parseFloat(row[12].replace(/["',]/g, '')) || 0;
                                let cess = parseFloat(row[13].replace(/["',]/g, '')) || 0;
                                
                                let totalTax = igst + cgst + sgst + cess;

                                records.push({ month: res.value.month, gstin, name, invNo, date, taxable, tax: totalTax });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    
                    if (records.length === 0) {
                        return Swal.fire("Error", "No valid invoices found. Ensure this is the correct B2B.csv from the GST Portal.", "error");
                    }

                    // Send the bulk data to the backend
                    showLoad();
                    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
                    
                    google.script.run
                    .withFailureHandler(err => {
                        // 🛟 CRASH NET 1: Catch network failures during Upload
                        hideLoad();
                        Swal.fire('Upload Timeout', 'The connection to the server timed out. Please check your internet or try a smaller CSV file.', 'error');
                    })
                    .withSuccessHandler(r => {
                        try {
                            const parsed = JSON.parse(r);
                            if(parsed.status === 'success') {
                                
                                // STEP 2: Chain the Auto-Reconciliation WITH its own Crash Nets
                                google.script.run
                                .withFailureHandler(err => {
                                    // 🛟 CRASH NET 2: Catch network failures during Auto-Match
                                    hideLoad();
                                    Swal.fire('Partial Success', 'Data uploaded successfully, but the Auto-Match engine timed out. You can run it manually from the dashboard.', 'warning');
                                    if(typeof loadData === 'function') loadData();
                                })
                                .withSuccessHandler(reconRes => {
                                    hideLoad();
                                    try {
                                        const rr = JSON.parse(reconRes);
                                        Swal.fire('Success', parsed.message + "<br><br>" + rr.message, 'success');
                                        if(typeof loadData === 'function') loadData();
                                    } catch (e) {
                                        Swal.fire('Upload Complete', 'Upload succeeded, but Google returned an unknown response during Auto-Match.', 'info');
                                    }
                                })
                                .runAutoReconciliation(token, res.value.month); // 👈 FIXED: Token is now passed first!

                            } else {
                                hideLoad();
                                Swal.fire('Database Error', parsed.message, 'error');
                            }
                        } catch (err) {
                            // 🛟 CRASH NET 3: Catch Google Server "502 Bad Gateway" HTML crashes
                            hideLoad();
                            console.error("Raw Google Response:", r);
                            Swal.fire('Data Overflow', 'The file was too massive and Google timed out. The system safely aborted.', 'error');
                        }
                    }).saveBulkGSTR2A(token, JSON.stringify(records));
                };
                reader.readAsText(res.value.file);
            }
        });
    }

    function openPay(i) {
        const inv = DATA.invoices[i];
        nav('payments');
        document.getElementById('form-payment').reset();
        // NEW: Force the collapsible form to open so the user can see it!
        document.getElementById('collapsePayment').classList.add('show');
        const s = document.getElementById('pay-inv-select');
        s.innerHTML = "<option value=''>Loading...</option>";
        const radio = document.querySelector('input[name="payType"][value="Invoice"]');
        if(radio) { radio.checked = true; togglePayType(); }

        const clientSelect = document.getElementById('pay-client');
        let foundIdx = "";
        const strictMatch = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === inv.company && (c.site || "Main Site") === (inv.siteName || "Main Site"));
        if(strictMatch) {
            clientSelect.value = strictMatch.idx;
            foundIdx = strictMatch.idx;
        } else {
            for (let x = 0; x < clientSelect.options.length; x++) {
                if (clientSelect.options[x].text.includes(inv.company)) {
                    clientSelect.selectedIndex = x;
                    foundIdx = clientSelect.value;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        document.getElementById('pay-amt').value = (inv.due || 0).toFixed(2);
        if(foundIdx !== "") {
            setTimeout(() => { checkPending(inv.invNum); }, 100);
        } else {
            Swal.fire('Warning', "Client not found in Client list. Please select manually.", 'warning');
            s.innerHTML = "<option value=''>Manual Selection Required</option>";
        }
    }

    function checkPending(targetInv) { 
        const idx = document.getElementById('pay-client').value;
        if (!idx && idx !== 0 && idx !== "0") return;
        const clientObj = DATA.clients.find(function(c) { return c.idx == idx; }); 
        if(!clientObj) return;
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            let l = JSON.parse(res); 
            let s = document.getElementById('pay-inv-select'); 
            s.innerHTML = ""; 
            let def = document.createElement("option"); 
            def.text = "Select Invoice"; def.value = ""; s.add(def);

            l.forEach(function(i) { 
                let o = document.createElement("option"); 
                o.value = i.invNum; 
                o.text = i.invNum + " (Due: ₹" + i.due + ")"; 
                s.add(o); 
            }); 
            if (targetInv) { 
                for (let y = 0; y < s.options.length; y++) { 
                    if (s.options[y].value === targetInv) { s.selectedIndex = y; break; } 
                } 
            } 
        }).getPendingInvoices(clientObj.name, clientObj.site);
    }

    function loadGSTR3B() { 
        const m = document.getElementById('rep-3b-month').value;
        const y = document.getElementById('rep-3b-year').value; showLoad();
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'); 
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            hideLoad(); const d = JSON.parse(res); 
            document.getElementById('3b-taxable').innerText = (d.taxable||0).toFixed(2); document.getElementById('3b-igst').innerText = (d.igst||0).toFixed(2); 
            document.getElementById('3b-cgst').innerText = (d.cgst||0).toFixed(2); document.getElementById('3b-sgst').innerText = (d.sgst||0).toFixed(2); 
            document.getElementById('3b-itc').innerText = (d.itc||0).toFixed(2);
            
            const outputTax = (d.igst||0) + (d.cgst||0) + (d.sgst||0);
            const inputTax = (d.itc||0);
            const net = outputTax - inputTax;
            const el = document.getElementById('3b-net-total');
            el.innerText = net.toFixed(2);
            el.className = net > 0 ? "text-danger fw-bold" : "text-success fw-bold"; 
        }).getAdvancedReports(token,"GSTR3B", {month:m, year:y});
    }

    function submitCostHead() {
        const name = document.getElementById('add-ch-name').value;
        const cat = document.getElementById('add-ch-cat').value;
        const site = document.getElementById('add-ch-site').value;
        if(!name) return Swal.fire('Info', 'Enter Cost Head Name', 'info');
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => {
            hideLoad();
            const res = JSON.parse(r);
            if(res.status === "error") return Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
            document.getElementById('add-ch-name').value = '';
            Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: res.message || 'Cost Head Added'});
            loadData();
        }).addCostHead(token,name, cat, site);
    }
    
    function manageInput(action, type) { 
        let inputId = 'add-sac';
        if(type === 'UNIT') inputId = 'add-unit'; 
        
        const el = document.getElementById(inputId);
        if(!el) return;
        const v = el.value.trim();
        if(action === 'add' && !v) return Swal.fire('Info', 'Enter Value to Add', 'info');
        if(action === 'delete' && !v) return Swal.fire('Info', 'Enter Value to Delete', 'info'); 
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => { 
            hideLoad(); 
            const res = JSON.parse(r); 
            if(res.status === "exists") { 
                Swal.fire('Error', "Value already exists!", 'error'); 
            } else if(res.status === "error") {
                 Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error'); 
            } else { 
                loadData(); 
                Toast.fire({icon:'success', title:'Updated'}); 
                el.value = ""; 
            } 
        }).manageInput(token, action, type, v);
    }

    function loadCostHeadReport() {
        const siteFilter = document.getElementById('ch-filter-site').value;
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
            hideLoad();
            const data = JSON.parse(res);
            const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-ch-pnl-body');
            tb.innerHTML = "";
            if(data.length === 0) {
                tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">No data found for any Cost Head</td></tr>';
                return;
            }
            data.forEach(r => {
                const color = r.profit >= 0 ? 'text-success' : 'text-danger';
                tb.innerHTML += `<tr><td class="fw-bold">${r.costHead}</td><td class="text-primary">₹${r.revenue.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td><td class="text-danger">₹${r.expense.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td><td class="text-warning">₹${r.adv.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td><td class="fw-bold ${color}">₹${r.profit.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td></tr>`;
            });
        }).getCostHeadReportData(siteFilter);
    }

    function loadLiability() { 
        showLoad();
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            hideLoad(); 
            const d = JSON.parse(res); 
            const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-liability-body'); 
            tb.innerHTML = ""; 
            
            d.forEach(r => { 
                const due = Math.max(0, (r.net || 0) - (r.paid || 0));
                
                // Enhanced Status Badge logic
                let status = "";
                if (r.net === 0 && r.closingITC > 0) {
                    status = `<span class="badge bg-info text-dark rounded-pill px-3">ITC Forwarded</span>`;
                } else if (due > 0) {
                    status = `<span class="badge bg-danger rounded-pill px-3">Unpaid</span>`;
                } else {
                    status = `<span class="badge bg-success rounded-pill px-3">Paid</span>`;
                }

                tb.innerHTML += `
                <tr>
                    <td class="fw-bold">${r.month}</td>
                    <td>₹${(parseFloat(r.liability) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td class="text-primary fw-bold">₹${(parseFloat(r.openingITC) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td class="text-success">₹${(parseFloat(r.itc) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td class="text-danger fw-bold">₹${(parseFloat(r.net) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td class="text-info fw-bold">₹${(parseFloat(r.closingITC) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td>₹${(parseFloat(r.paid) || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td class="fw-bold text-dark">₹${due.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    <td>${status}</td>
                </tr>`; 
            }); 
        }).getAdvancedReports(token,"Liability", {});
    }

    function renderSalesReport() { 
        if(!DATA.invoices) return; 
        
        // PREMIUM FIX: Dynamically populate Sales Analysis Month Dropdown based on real data
        const mSelect = document.getElementById('sales-month-filter');
        if (mSelect && mSelect.options.length <= 1) {
            let months = new Set();
            DATA.invoices.forEach(i => {
                if(i.date && i.status !== 'Cancelled') {
                    let d = new Date(i.date);
                    if(!isNaN(d)) months.add(d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'));
                }
            });
            [...months].sort().reverse().forEach(m => {
                let [yr, mo] = m.split('-');
                let name = new Date(yr, mo-1).toLocaleString('default', { month: 'short' });
                mSelect.add(new Option(`${name} ${yr}`, m));
            });
        }
        
        const group = document.getElementById('sales-group') ? document.getElementById('sales-group').value : 'client'; 
        const mFilter = document.getElementById('sales-month-filter') ? document.getElementById('sales-month-filter').value : ''; 
        const salesMap = {}; let grandTotal = 0;
        DATA.invoices.forEach(function(inv) {
            if (inv.type === 'Tax Invoice' && inv.status !== 'Cancelled') {
                if (mFilter && !inv.date.startsWith(mFilter)) return;
                const key = group === 'client' ? inv.company : (inv.siteName || "Main Site");
                salesMap[key] = (salesMap[key] || 0) + (inv.total || 0);
                grandTotal += (inv.total || 0);
            }
        });
        const sorted = Object.entries(salesMap).sort(function(a, b) { return b[1] - a[1]; });
        const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-sales-body');
        if (tb) {
            tb.innerHTML = sorted.map(function(item) {
                const key = item[0];
                const total = item[1];
                const count = DATA.invoices.filter(function(i) {
                    const iKey = group === 'client' ? i.company : (i.siteName || "Main Site");
                    return iKey === key && i.type === 'Tax Invoice' && i.status !== 'Cancelled' && (mFilter ? i.date.startsWith(mFilter) : true);
                }).length;
                return '<tr><td>' + key + '</td><td><span class="badge bg-secondary rounded-pill">' + count + '</span></td><td class="fw-bold text-success">₹' + total.toLocaleString('en-IN') + '</td></tr>';
            }).join('');
            
            tb.innerHTML += '<tr class="table-dark fw-bold"><td style="color: white !important;">TOTAL</td><td style="color: white !important;">-</td><td style="color: white !important;">₹' + grandTotal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) + '</td></tr>';
        }
    }
    
    function loadLedger() { const party = document.getElementById('led-client').value; const site = document.getElementById('led-site').value; const start = document.getElementById('led-start').value; const end = document.getElementById('led-end').value; const isVendor = document.getElementById('led-type').value === 'Vendor'; if(!party) return Swal.fire('Info', "Select a Party", 'info'); showLoad();
 // 1. ADDED: Grab the token right before the checks
 const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');

 if(isVendor) { 
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
        hideLoad(); 
        let d = JSON.parse(res); 
        let tb = document.getElementById('tbl-led'); 
        let bal = 0; 
        tb.innerHTML = ""; 
        if(d.length === 0) { 
            tb.innerHTML = "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center'>No payments found</td></tr>"; 
            if(document.getElementById('ledger-summary')) document.getElementById('ledger-summary').classList.add('d-none'); 
            return; 
        } 
        d.forEach(function(r) { 
            bal += r.debit; 
            tb.innerHTML += '<tr><td>' + r.date + '</td><td>' + r.desc + '</td><td class="text-dark">₹' + (r.debit || 0) + '</td><td class="text-secondary">-</td><td class="fw-bold">₹' + bal.toFixed(2) + ' (Paid)</td></tr>'; 
        }); 
        if(document.getElementById('ledger-summary')) { 
            document.getElementById('ledger-summary').classList.remove('d-none'); 
            document.getElementById('led-sum-party').innerText = party + (site ? ' (' + site + ')' : ''); 
            document.getElementById('led-sum-close').innerText = '₹' + Math.abs(bal).toFixed(2) + ' (Paid)'; 
        } 
    }).getVendorLedger(token, party, start, end, site); // 👈 2. ADDED: 'token,' passed as first parameter
 } else { 
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
        hideLoad(); 
        let d = JSON.parse(res); 
        let tb = document.getElementById('tbl-led'); 
        let bal = 0; 
        tb.innerHTML = ""; 
        if(d.length === 0) { 
            tb.innerHTML = "<tr><td colspan='5' class='text-center'>No records found</td></tr>"; 
            if(document.getElementById('ledger-summary')) document.getElementById('ledger-summary').classList.add('d-none'); 
            return; 
        } 
        d.forEach(function(r) { 
            const dr = parseFloat(r.debit || 0); 
            const cr = parseFloat(r.credit || 0); 
            bal += (dr - cr); 
            let balText = bal < 0 ? "(Adv)" : "(Due)"; 
            tb.innerHTML += `<tr><td>${r.date}</td><td>${r.desc}</td><td class="text-dark">${dr > 0 ? '₹' + dr.toFixed(2) : '-'}</td><td class="text-dark">${cr > 0 ? '₹' + cr.toFixed(2) : '-'}</td><td class="fw-bold ${balText === '(Due)' ? 'text-danger' : 'text-success'}">₹${Math.abs(bal).toFixed(2)} <small>${balText}</small></td></tr>`; 
        }); 
        if(document.getElementById('ledger-summary')) { 
            document.getElementById('ledger-summary').classList.remove('d-none'); 
            document.getElementById('led-sum-party').innerText = party + (site ? ' (' + site + ')' : ''); 
            document.getElementById('led-sum-close').innerText = '₹' + Math.abs(bal).toFixed(2) + (bal < 0 ? ' (Adv)' : ' (Due)'); 
        } 
    }).getLedgerData(token, party, site, start, end); // 👈 3. ADDED: 'token,' passed as first parameter
 } }

    
    function loadSitePnL() {
        const site = document.getElementById('pnl-site-select').value;
        if(!site) return Swal.fire('Info', "Select a site", 'info'); 
        showLoad();
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            hideLoad(); 
            const d = JSON.parse(res); 
            const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-pnl-body'); 
            const color = d.profit >= 0 ? 'text-success' : 'text-danger'; 
            
            // 1. Draw the Main Site Totals (Same data, consistent font)
            let html = `<tr class="fw-bold bg-light fs-6">
                <td class="text-uppercase text-primary">${d.site}</td>
                <td class="text-success">₹${d.income.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="text-danger">₹${d.vendor.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="text-warning">₹${d.gst.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="text-info">₹${d.tds.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="${color}">₹${d.profit.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
            </tr>`;

            // 2. NEW FLAT DESIGN: Draw the Work Order Breakdown directly into the main grid
            if (d.workOrders && d.workOrders.length > 0) {
                // We use 6 exact columns here so the CSV export aligns perfectly!
                html += `<tr class="table-secondary text-muted text-uppercase fw-bold" style="font-size: 0.75rem;">
                    <td class="text-start ps-4"><i class="bi bi-diagram-2 me-1"></i> Work Order Breakdown</td>
                    <td>Gross Revenue Billed</td>
                    <td>Direct Expenses</td>
                    <td></td> <td></td> <td>Est. Gross Margin</td>
                </tr>`;
                
                d.workOrders.forEach(w => {
                    let wColor = w.profit >= 0 ? 'text-success' : 'text-danger';
                    html += `<tr>
                        <td class="fw-bold text-secondary text-start ps-5"><i class="bi bi-arrow-return-right me-2 text-muted"></i>${w.woNo}</td>
                        <td class="text-dark fw-bold">₹${w.revenue.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                        <td class="text-danger">₹${w.cost.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                        <td></td> <td></td> <td class="fw-bold ${wColor}">₹${w.profit.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                    </tr>`;
                });
            }

            tb.innerHTML = html; 
        }).getSitePnL(token,site);
    }
    
    function saveRecon() { 
        const month = document.getElementById('recon-month').value; 
        const itc = document.getElementById('recon-itc').value;
        if(!month || !itc) return Swal.fire('Info', "Enter Month and ITC Amount", 'info'); 
        
        showLoad();

        // ENTERPRISE FIX: Grab the security token!
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        
        google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler((res)=>{ 
                hideLoad(); 
                let r = JSON.parse(res);
                if(r.status === "success") {
                    Swal.fire('Success', 'ITC Saved Successfully', 'success'); 
                    if(typeof loadLiability === 'function') loadLiability(); 
                } else {
                    Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
                }
            })
            // ENTERPRISE FIX: Send BOTH the token and the data
            .saveGSTR2A(token, JSON.stringify(d)); 
    }
    
    async function savePayment(e) { 
    e.preventDefault();

    // 🛑 1. GRAB BUTTON AND DISABLE IT IMMEDIATELY
    const saveBtn = document.querySelector('#form-payment button[type="submit"]') || document.getElementById('btn-save-payment');
    let originalText = "Save"; 
    if(saveBtn) {
        originalText = saveBtn.innerHTML; // Remember what the button originally said
        saveBtn.disabled = true;
        saveBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Saving...';
    }

    showLoad(); 
    
    // Capture form values safely
    const pType = document.querySelector('input[name="payType"]:checked').value;
    const ptInv = document.getElementById('pt-inv').checked; 
    
    let rawAmt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pay-amt').value) || 0;
    let amt = Math.abs(rawAmt); 
    const flow = document.querySelector('input[name="payFlow"]:checked').value;
    
    let clientName = "";
    let siteName = ""; 
    let ref = "";
    let expenseHead = document.getElementById('pay-head') ? document.getElementById('pay-head').value : "";
    
    // 🚀 THE FIX: SMART WORK ORDER REFERENCE
    if (pType === "Invoice" || ptInv) {
        ref = document.getElementById('pay-inv-select').value;
    } else {
        const woSelect = document.getElementById('pay-wo');
        const customRef = document.getElementById('pay-custom-ref').value;
        // If Work Order is selected, use it. Otherwise, use custom reference.
        ref = (woSelect && woSelect.value !== "") ? woSelect.value : customRef;
    }
    
    if(pType === "Vendor Payment") { 
        clientName = document.getElementById('pay-client').value;
        siteName = document.getElementById('pay-site').value; 
        if(flow === 'out') amt = -Math.abs(amt); else amt = Math.abs(amt);
    } else { 
        const idx = document.getElementById('pay-client').value;
        if(!idx && idx !== 0 && idx !== "0") { 
            hideLoad(); 
            if(saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = false; saveBtn.innerHTML = originalText; }
            return Swal.fire('Info', "Select Client", 'info');
        } 
        
        const clientObj = DATA.clients.find(function(c) { return c.idx == idx; });
        if(clientObj) {
            clientName = clientObj.name; 
            siteName = clientObj.site; 
        }
        if(flow === 'out') amt = -Math.abs(amt); else amt = Math.abs(amt);
    } 
    
    // Handle File Uploads
    let receiptUrl = "";
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('pay-receipt-file');
    if(fileInput && fileInput.files.length > 0) {
        const file = fileInput.files[0];
        try {
            const base64 = await getBase64(file);
            const uploadRes = await new Promise(resolve => {
                google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(res => resolve(JSON.parse(res)))
                .uploadPaymentReceipt(base64, file.name, file.type);
            });
            if(uploadRes.status === 'success') receiptUrl = uploadRes.url;
        } catch(err) { 
            console.error("File upload failed", err); 
        }
    }

    // 🚀 THE FIX: UNIFIED PAYLOAD DATA
    const d = {
        id: document.getElementById('pay-id').value,
        clientName: clientName,
        site: siteName,
        type: pType,
        flow: flow,
        date: document.getElementById('pay-date').value,
        mode: document.getElementById('pay-mode').value,
        amount: amt,
        head: expenseHead,
        ref: ref, 
        challan: "", 
        lastModified: document.getElementById('pay-lastModified').value, 
        receiptUrl: receiptUrl,
        woNo: document.getElementById('pay-wo') ? document.getElementById('pay-wo').value : ""
    };

    // 🔒 Fetch Token
    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');

    google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
            const response = JSON.parse(res);
            if(response.status === "error") {
                hideLoad();
                if(saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = false; saveBtn.innerHTML = originalText; }
                return Swal.fire('Error', response.message, 'error');
            }
            
            if(typeof silentSync === 'function') {
                silentSync(() => { 
                    hideLoad(); 
                    if(typeof Toast !== 'undefined') Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: 'Payment Saved'}); 
                    
                    document.getElementById('form-payment').reset(); 
                    document.getElementById('pay-id').value = ""; 
                    document.getElementById('pay-lastModified').value = ""; 
                    document.getElementById('pay-inv-select').innerHTML = ""; 
                    document.getElementById('pf-in').checked = true; 
                    document.getElementById('pt-adv').checked = true; 
                    if(typeof togglePayType === 'function') togglePayType(); 
                    if(fileInput) fileInput.value = "";

                    if(saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = false; saveBtn.innerHTML = originalText; }
                });
            }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
            hideLoad();
            console.error("Save Payment Error:", err);
            if(saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = false; saveBtn.innerHTML = originalText; }
            Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || 'Failed to save payment.', 'error');
        })
        .savePayment(token, JSON.stringify(d)); 
}

    function openBulkPaymentModal() {
        document.getElementById('formBulkPayment').reset();
        document.getElementById('bpDate').value = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA');
        document.getElementById('bpPendingAlert').style.display = 'none';
        new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('modalBulkPayment')).show();
    }

    function checkPendingForBulk() {
        const client = document.getElementById('bpClient').value;
        if(!client) return;
        let dueCount = 0; let dueAmt = 0;
        DATA.invoices.forEach(i => {
            if(i.company === client && i.type === "Tax Invoice" && i.status !== "Cancelled" && i.due > 0) {
                dueCount++; dueAmt += i.due;
            }
        });
        const alert = document.getElementById('bpPendingAlert');
        if(dueCount > 0) {
            alert.style.display = 'block';
            alert.innerHTML = `<i class="bi bi-info-circle me-2"></i>Found <b>${dueCount}</b> pending invoices totaling <b>₹${dueAmt.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</b>.`;
        } else {
            alert.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }

    async function submitBulkPayment(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveBulkPay'); btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='Processing...';
        showLoad();
        
        let receiptUrl = "";
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('bpReceiptFile');
        if(fileInput && fileInput.files.length > 0) {
            const file = fileInput.files[0];
            try {
                const base64 = await getBase64(file);
                const uploadRes = await new Promise(resolve => {
                    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => resolve(JSON.parse(res))).uploadPaymentReceipt(base64, file.name, file.type);
                });
                if(uploadRes.status === 'success') receiptUrl = uploadRes.url;
            } catch(err) { console.error("File upload failed", err); }
        }

        const cData = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === document.getElementById('bpClient').value);
        const payload = {
            clientName: document.getElementById('bpClient').value,
            site: cData ? cData.site : "Main Site",
            date: document.getElementById('bpDate').value,
            amount: document.getElementById('bpAmount').value,
            mode: document.getElementById('bpMode').value,
            receiptUrl: receiptUrl
        };

        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
            btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='Process Allocation';
            hideLoad();
            const p = JSON.parse(res);
            if(p.status === 'success') {
                const modal = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('modalBulkPayment'));
                if(modal) modal.hide();
                Swal.fire('Allocation Complete', p.message, 'success');
                silentSync();
            } else {
                Swal.fire('Error', p.message, 'error');
            }
        }).processBulkPayment(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), JSON.stringify(payload));
    }
    
    function switchFinanceTab(t) { 
        document.querySelectorAll('.finance-section').forEach(function(d) { d.style.display = 'none'; });
        document.getElementById('finance-sec-' + t).style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelectorAll('#finance-tabs .nav-link').forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); });
        event.target.classList.add('active');
    }

    function renderFinancials() {
        if(userRole !== 'admin') return;
        document.getElementById('tfoot-tb').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="text-center py-3"><div class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm text-primary"></div></td></tr>';
        
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(res);
            if(parsed.status !== "success") { Swal.fire('Error', parsed.message, 'error'); return; }
            
            const tbBody = document.querySelector('#table-tb tbody');
            let tDr=0, tCr=0;
            tbBody.innerHTML = parsed.trialBalance.map(a => {
                const dr = a.net > 0 ? a.net : 0;
                const cr = a.net < 0 ? Math.abs(a.net) : 0;
                tDr += dr; tCr += cr;
                return `<tr><td class="fw-bold text-dark">${a.account}</td><td class="text-end text-success">${dr>0?'₹'+dr.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}):'-'}</td><td class="text-end text-danger">${cr>0?'₹'+cr.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}):'-'}</td></tr>`;
            }).join('');
            document.getElementById('tfoot-tb').innerHTML = `<tr><td class="text-end">Total:</td><td class="text-end text-success fs-5">₹${tDr.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td><td class="text-end text-danger fs-5">₹${tCr.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td></tr>`;

            const bsAssets = document.querySelector('#table-bs-assets tbody');
            let tA = 0;
            bsAssets.innerHTML = parsed.balanceSheet.assets.map(a => { tA+=a.value; return `<tr><td>${a.name}</td><td class="text-end fw-bold">₹${a.value.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td></tr>`; }).join('');
            document.getElementById('total-assets').innerText = '₹' + tA.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});

            const bsLiab = document.querySelector('#table-bs-liab tbody');
            let tL = 0;
            bsLiab.innerHTML = parsed.balanceSheet.liabilities.map(a => { tL+=a.value; return `<tr><td>${a.name}</td><td class="text-end fw-bold">₹${a.value.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td></tr>`; }).join('');
            
            let eqHtml = '';
            parsed.balanceSheet.equity.forEach(e => {
                tL += (e.isLoss ? -e.value : e.value);
                eqHtml += `<tr class="${e.isLoss ? 'text-danger' : 'text-success'}"><td>${e.name}</td><td class="text-end fw-bold">${e.isLoss?'- ':''}₹${Math.abs(e.value).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td></tr>`;
            });
            bsLiab.innerHTML += '<tr><td colspan="2" class="p-0"><hr class="my-1"></td></tr>' + eqHtml;
            document.getElementById('total-liab').innerText = '₹' + tL.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});

        }).getFinancialStatements();
    }

    function addJournalRow() {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><input type="text" class="form-control form-control-sm jr-acc" placeholder="e.g. Depreciation Expense" required></td>
            <td><input type="number" step="0.01" class="form-control form-control-sm jr-dr" value="0" oninput="calcJournal()"></td>
            <td><input type="number" step="0.01" class="form-control form-control-sm jr-cr" value="0" oninput="calcJournal()"></td>
            <td><button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove();calcJournal()"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></button></td>
        `;
        document.getElementById('journalBody').appendChild(tr);
    }

    function calcJournal() {
        let dr = 0, cr = 0;
        document.querySelectorAll('#journalBody tr').forEach(tr => {
            dr += parseFloat(tr.querySelector('.jr-dr').value) || 0;
            cr += parseFloat(tr.querySelector('.jr-cr').value) || 0;
        });
        document.getElementById('jrTotalDr').innerText = dr.toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById('jrTotalCr').innerText = cr.toFixed(2);
        
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveJournal');
        const warn = document.getElementById('jrWarning');
        if(Math.abs(dr - cr) > 0.01 || dr === 0) {
            btn.disabled = true; warn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle text-danger"></i> Debits must equal Credits';
        } else {
            btn.disabled = false; warn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-check-circle text-success"></i> Balanced';
        }
    }

    function submitJournal(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const entries = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('#journalBody tr').forEach(tr => {
            entries.push({
                acc: tr.querySelector('.jr-acc').value,
                dr: parseFloat(tr.querySelector('.jr-dr').value) || 0,
                cr: parseFloat(tr.querySelector('.jr-cr').value) || 0
            });
        });
        const payload = { date: document.getElementById('jrDate').value, entries: entries };
        
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveJournal'); btn.disabled = true;
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res => {
            hideLoad();
            const p = JSON.parse(res);
            if(p.status === 'success') { 
                Swal.fire('Posted', p.message, 'success'); 
                document.getElementById('journalBody').innerHTML=''; 
                addJournalRow(); addJournalRow(); 
                renderFinancials(); 
            }
            else Swal.fire('Error', p.message, 'error');
        }).saveManualJournal(JSON.stringify(payload));
    }

    function checkStateCode(state, gstin) {
        if(!gstin || gstin.length < 2) return true;
        const code = gstin.substring(0, 2);
        const map = {
            "Andhra Pradesh": "37", "Telangana": "36", "Karnataka": "29", "Tamil Nadu": "33", 
            "Maharashtra": "27", "Delhi": "07", "Gujarat": "24","Uttar Pradesh": "09","Haryana": "06"
        };
        if(map[state] && map[state] !== code) {
            return confirm(`Warning: State '${state}' usually has GST code '${map[state]}', but GSTIN starts with '${code}'. Continue?`);
        }
        return true;
    }

    function validateGSTIN(gstin) {
        if(!gstin) return true;
        const regex = /^[0-9]{2}[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]{1}[1-9A-Z]{1}Z[0-9A-Z]{1}$/;
        return regex.test(gstin);
    }
    
    function saveClient(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    const gstin = document.getElementById('c-gstin').value.toUpperCase();
    const state = document.getElementById('c-state').value;
    
    if(gstin && !validateGSTIN(gstin)) {
         return Swal.fire('Invalid GSTIN', 'Please check the GSTIN format.', 'error');
    }
    if(!checkStateCode(state, gstin)) return;

    showLoad(); // Starts the loading spinner
    
    // 1. Fetch the security token from browser storage
    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'); 

    const d = {
        id: document.getElementById('c-id').value, 
        name: document.getElementById('c-name').value, 
        gstin: gstin, 
        state: state, 
        city: document.getElementById('c-city').value, 
        address: document.getElementById('c-addr').value, 
        phone: document.getElementById('c-phone').value, 
        email: document.getElementById('c-email').value, 
        site: document.getElementById('c-site').value
    };
    
    google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler((r) => {
             hideLoad(); 
             const res = JSON.parse(r);
             if (res.status === "error") return Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
             closeModal('modal-client'); 
             silentSync(); 
             Toast.fire({icon:'success', title:'Client Saved'}); 
        })
        // 2. Add the Crash Net to catch server/token rejections
        .withFailureHandler((err) => {
             hideLoad(); // Stops the spinner so the screen doesn't freeze
             console.error("Save Client Error:", err);
             Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || 'Failed to save client. Please log in again.', 'error');
        })
        // 3. Pass the token FIRST, then the JSON string
        .saveClient(token, JSON.stringify(d)); 
}
    
    
    // ==========================================
    // SECURE RECORD DELETION (WITH TYPE-TO-CONFIRM)
    // ==========================================
    function del(t, i) {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Are you sure?',
            html: `You are about to delete a record from <b>${t}</b>.<br><br>To confirm, type <b>DELETE</b> below:`,
            input: 'text',
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
            cancelButtonColor: '#3085d6',
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Delete it',
            inputValidator: (value) => {
                if (value !== 'DELETE') {
                    return 'You must type DELETE exactly (all uppercase) to proceed!';
                }
            }
        }).then((result) => {
            if (result.isConfirmed) {
                showLoad();
                
                const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
                if(!token) {
                    hideLoad();
                    return Swal.fire('Error', 'Session missing. Please log out and back in.', 'error');
                }
                
                google.script.run
                    .withSuccessHandler((resStr) => {
                        hideLoad();
                        const r = JSON.parse(resStr);
                        if (r.status === "error") {
                            // Only log out if it's an actual Unauthorized/Session error
                            if (r.message && (r.message.includes('Unauthorized') || r.message.includes('expired'))) {
                                sessionStorage.removeItem('loggedIn');
                                sessionStorage.removeItem('sessionToken');
                                document.body.classList.remove('logged-in');
                                document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.opacity = 1;
                                document.getElementById('login-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
                                document.getElementById('step-1-pin').style.display = 'block';
                                document.getElementById('step-2-otp').style.display = 'none';
                                Swal.fire('Session Expired', 'For security, your 6-hour session has ended. Please log in again.', 'warning');
                            } else {
                                // Show normal guardrail blocks (e.g., "Cannot delete paid invoice")
                                Swal.fire('Blocked', r.message, 'error');
                            }
                        } else {
                            loadData();
                            Toast.fire({icon:'success', title:'Record Deleted Successfully'});
                        }
                    })
                    .withFailureHandler((err) => {
                        hideLoad();
                        Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || err, 'error');
                    })
                    .deleteData(token, t, i); // Secure backend call
            }
        });
    }
    
    function togglePayType(){ 
        const pVal = document.querySelector('input[name="payType"]:checked').value;
        const isVendor = pVal === "Vendor Payment"; const isInv = pVal === "Invoice";
        document.getElementById('pay-amt').value = ""; document.getElementById('pay-inv-select').innerHTML = "";
        const payClient = document.getElementById('pay-client'); const payClientWrapper = payClient.parentElement; const siteGroup = document.getElementById('pay-site-group');
        payClientWrapper.style.display = 'block';
        if(isVendor) { 
            payClient.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Vendor...</option>';
            if(DATA.vendors) DATA.vendors.forEach(v => payClient.add(new Option(v.name, v.name)));
            siteGroup.style.display = 'flex'; 
        } else { 
            payClient.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Client...</option>';
            if(DATA.clients) DATA.clients.forEach(c => payClient.add(new Option(c.name + " - " + c.site, c.idx))); 
            siteGroup.style.display = 'none';
        }
        document.getElementById('pay-inv-group').style.display=isInv?'block':'none'; document.getElementById('pay-flow-group').style.display='block';
    }
    // 1. The Master UI Email Composer with loading spinner
    function fireEmailComposerSwal(invNum, pdfUrl, clientName, defaultEmail) {
        Swal.fire({
            title: '<i class="bi bi-envelope-paper me-2 text-primary"></i>Compose Email',
            html: `
                <div class="text-start mt-3">
                    <label class="form-label small fw-bold text-muted mb-1">To:</label>
                    <input id="swal-ev-to" class="form-control mb-3 border-primary" value="${defaultEmail}" placeholder="veeranki97@gmail.com">
                    
                    <label class="form-label small fw-bold text-muted mb-1">Subject:</label>
                    <input id="swal-ev-sub" class="form-control mb-3 border-primary" value="Invoice ${invNum} from Sai Durga">
                    
                    <label class="form-label small fw-bold text-muted mb-1">Message Body:</label>
                    <textarea id="swal-ev-msg" class="form-control border-primary" rows="6" style="font-size:0.9rem;">Dear ${clientName},\n\nPlease find attached your invoice (${invNum}). You can also view and download it directly using the secure link below:\n\n${pdfUrl}\n\nThank you for your business.\n\nRegards,\nSai Durga</textarea>
                </div>
            `,
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-send-fill me-1"></i> Send Email',
            confirmButtonColor: '#007aff',
            cancelButtonColor: '#6c757d',
            customClass: { popup: 'rounded-4' },
            preConfirm: () => {
                const to = document.getElementById('swal-ev-to').value;
                const sub = document.getElementById('swal-ev-sub').value;
                const msg = document.getElementById('swal-ev-msg').value;
                if (!to) { Swal.showValidationMessage('Recipient email is required'); return false; }
                return { to, sub, msg };
            }
        }).then((result) => {
            if (result.isConfirmed) {
                // Replaces the global screen dimming with an integrated loading spinner
                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Sending Email...',
                    html: 'Attaching PDF and dispatching, please wait.',
                    allowOutsideClick: false,
                    didOpen: () => { Swal.showLoading(); }
                });

                const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'); // 🔑 FETCH TOKEN HERE

                google.script.run
                    .withSuccessHandler(function(serverRes) {
                        const res = JSON.parse(serverRes);
                        if (res.status === 'success') Swal.fire({icon: 'success', title: 'Sent!', text: res.message, timer: 3000});
                        else Swal.fire({icon: 'error', title: 'Email Failed', text: res.message});
                    })
                    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
                        Swal.fire({icon: 'error', title: 'Server Error', text: error.toString()});
                    })
                    .sendInvoiceEmail(token, JSON.stringify({ // 🔑 PASS TOKEN HERE AS FIRST VARIABLE
                        invNum: invNum, 
                        pdf: pdfUrl, 
                        email: result.value.to, 
                        subject: result.value.sub, 
                        body: result.value.msg 
                    }));
            }
        });
    }

    // 2. Triggered from the 3-Dots Menu
    function quickEmailInv(i) {
        const inv = DATA.invoices[i];
        if(!inv.pdf || inv.pdf.includes('#')) return Swal.fire('Error', "PDF not generated yet. Edit and Save to generate.", 'warning');
        const clientRecord = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === inv.company && (c.site || "Main Site") === (inv.siteName || "Main Site"));
        const email = clientRecord ? clientRecord.email : "";
        
        fireEmailComposerSwal(inv.invNum, inv.pdf, inv.company, email);
    }

    // 3. Triggered from inside the Invoice Editor
    function sendEmailInv() {
        const n = document.getElementById('i-no').value;
        const linkEl = document.getElementById('i-pdf-link');
        const p = linkEl ? linkEl.href : "";
        const clientName = document.getElementById('i-company').value;
        const siteSelect = document.getElementById('i-site-select');
        const siteName = siteSelect.options[siteSelect.selectedIndex] ? siteSelect.options[siteSelect.selectedIndex].text : "Main Site";

        if (!p || p === "" || p.includes("#")) {
            return Swal.fire({ icon: 'warning', title: 'PDF Not Ready', text: 'Please SAVE the invoice first.' });
        }

        const clientObj = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === clientName && (c.site || "Main Site") === (siteName || "Main Site"));
        const email = clientObj ? clientObj.email : "";

        // BULLETPROOF FIX: Physically hide the Bootstrap modal to destroy the Focus Trap
        const modalEl = document.getElementById('modal-inv');
        const modalInst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
        if (modalInst) {
            modalInst.hide();
        }

        // Wait 400ms for the Bootstrap closing animation to completely finish, THEN open the email composer
        setTimeout(() => {
            fireEmailComposerSwal(n, p, clientName, email);
        }, 400);
    }
   
    function editClient(i) {
        const c = DATA.clients[i];
        
        // 1. Populate the form
        document.getElementById('c-id').value = c.id; 
        document.getElementById('c-name').value = c.name; 
        document.getElementById('c-site').value = c.site || ""; 
        document.getElementById('c-gstin').value = c.gstin || "";
        document.getElementById('c-state').value = c.state || "Andhra Pradesh"; 
        document.getElementById('c-city').value = c.city || ""; 
        document.getElementById('c-email').value = c.email || "";
        document.getElementById('c-phone').value = c.phone || ""; 
        document.getElementById('c-addr').value = c.address || ""; 

        // 2. 🛑 GUARDIAN PROTOCOL (SITE-AWARE)
        let hasActiveInvoices = false;
        if (DATA && DATA.invoices) {
            // Safely grab the client's site, defaulting to empty if it doesn't exist
            const targetSite = (c.site || "").trim().toLowerCase();

            for (let j = 0; j < DATA.invoices.length; j++) {
                let inv = DATA.invoices[j];
                let invSite = (inv.siteName || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                
                // EXACT MATCH REQUIRED: Must match BOTH Company Name AND Site Name
                if (inv.company === c.name && invSite === targetSite && !String(inv.status).toUpperCase().includes("CANCELLED")) {
                    hasActiveInvoices = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Lock core financial fields if active invoices exist for THIS specific site
        const nameField = document.getElementById('c-name');
        const gstinField = document.getElementById('c-gstin');
        const stateField = document.getElementById('c-state');

        if (hasActiveInvoices) {
            nameField.disabled = true;
            gstinField.disabled = true;
            stateField.disabled = true;
            
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Restricted Edit',
                text: 'This specific Company + Site has active invoices. Core financial details are locked to preserve audit trails.',
                icon: 'info',
                toast: true,
                position: 'top-end',
                showConfirmButton: false,
                timer: 5000
            });
        } else {
            // Unlock fields if no active invoices for this site
            nameField.disabled = false;
            gstinField.disabled = false;
            stateField.disabled = false;
        }

        showModal('modal-client');
    }
    function copyClient(i){editClient(i); document.getElementById('c-id').value="";
        Toast.fire({icon:'info', title:'Copied. Modify and Save.'});
    }

    function editVendor(i) { const v = DATA.vendors[i];
        document.getElementById('v-id').value = v.id;
        document.getElementById('v-name').value = v.name; document.getElementById('v-gstin').value = v.gstin; document.getElementById('v-phone').value = v.phone; document.getElementById('v-email').value = v.email; document.getElementById('v-addr').value = v.address; showModal('modal-vendor');
    }
    
    function editPayment(i) { 
        const p = DATA.payments[i];
        
        // FIX: Force the collapsible payment form to open so it becomes visible!
        document.getElementById('collapsePayment').classList.add('show');
        
        document.getElementById('pay-id').value = p.id; 
        document.getElementById('pay-lastModified').value = p.lastModified || "";
        
        let displayRef = p.ref;
        if (displayRef === "-") displayRef = "";
        document.getElementById('pay-custom-ref').value = displayRef;
        // Restore the Work Order link in the Dropdown
        const woSelect = document.getElementById('pay-wo');
          if (woSelect) {
        let found = Array.from(woSelect.options).some(opt => opt.value === p.ref);
        woSelect.value = found ? p.ref : "";
          }

        if(p.type === "Vendor Payment") { 
            document.querySelector('input[name="payType"][value="Vendor Payment"]').checked = true;
            togglePayType(); 
            const vSelect = document.getElementById('pay-client'); 
            vSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Vendor...</option>';
            if(DATA.vendors) { DATA.vendors.forEach(v => { vSelect.add(new Option(v.name, v.name)); });
            } 
            vSelect.value = p.client; 
            const sSelect = document.getElementById('pay-site');
            if(sSelect) sSelect.value = p.site || ""; 
            const hInput = document.getElementById('pay-head'); 
            if(hInput && p.head) hInput.value = p.head; 
            document.getElementById('pay-date').value = p.date;
            document.getElementById('pay-amt').value = Math.abs(p.amount); 
            document.getElementById('pay-mode').value = p.mode; 
            document.getElementById('pf-out').checked = true; 
            document.getElementById('form-payment').scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); 
            return;
        } 
        
        const clientSelect = document.getElementById('pay-client');
        for (let x = 0; x < clientSelect.options.length; x++) { 
            if (clientSelect.options[x].text.includes(p.client)) { clientSelect.selectedIndex = x;
            break; } 
        } 
        document.querySelector('input[name="payType"][value="Advance"]').checked = true;
        document.getElementById('pay-date').value = p.date; 
        document.getElementById('pay-amt').value = Math.abs(p.amount); 
        document.getElementById('pay-mode').value = p.mode;
        if (parseFloat(p.amount) < 0) document.getElementById('pf-out').checked = true; else document.getElementById('pf-in').checked = true;
        
        document.getElementById('form-payment').scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); 
        Toast.fire({icon:'info', title:'Editing Payment'});
    }

    function cancelInv(id) { 
        Swal.fire({title: 'Cancel Invoice?', text: "Type 'CANCEL' to confirm:", input: 'text', icon:'warning', showCancelButton:true}).then((result)=>{
            if(result.value === 'CANCEL') { showLoad(); google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
                hideLoad(); 
                let r = JSON.parse(res);
            
                if(r.status === "error") Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
                else { loadData(); Swal.fire('Cancelled', 'Invoice Cancelled & Journal Reversed', 'success'); }
            }).cancelInvoice(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), id); }
        });
    }
    
    function restoreInv(id) { 
        Swal.fire({title: 'Restore Invoice?', text: "Status will revert to Pending/Partial.", icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true}).then((result)=>{
            if(result.isConfirmed) { showLoad(); google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { 
                hideLoad(); 
                let r = JSON.parse(res);
                
                if(r.status === "error") Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
                else { loadData(); Swal.fire('Restored', 'Invoice Restored & Journal Posted', 'success'); }
            }).restoreInvoice(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), id); }
        });
    }
    
    function cleanLogs() { 
        const val = document.getElementById('adm-log-days').value;
        const days = parseInt(val) || 30;
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        Swal.fire({title: 'Clean Logs?', text: `Delete logs older than ${days} days?`, icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true}).then((result)=>{
            if(result.isConfirmed) { showLoad(); google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) { hideLoad(); Swal.fire('Cleaned', res, 'success'); }).cleanAuditLog(token,days); }
        });
    }

    function numToWords(n) { const a = ['', 'One ', 'Two ', 'Three ', 'Four ', 'Five ', 'Six ', 'Seven ', 'Eight ', 'Nine ', 'Ten ', 'Eleven ', 'Twelve ', 'Thirteen ', 'Fourteen ', 'Fifteen ', 'Sixteen ', 'Seventeen ', 'Eighteen ', 'Nineteen '];
        const b = ['', '', 'Twenty', 'Thirty', 'Forty', 'Fifty', 'Sixty', 'Seventy', 'Eighty', 'Ninety'];
        if ((n = n.toString()).length > 9) return 'overflow'; n = ('000000000' + n).substr(-9).match(/^(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{1})(\d{2})$/); if (!n) return; var str = '';
        str += (n[1] != 0) ? (a[Number(n[1])] || b[n[1][0]] + ' ' + a[n[1][1]]) + 'Crore ' : '';
        str += (n[2] != 0) ? (a[Number(n[2])] || b[n[2][0]] + ' ' + a[n[2][1]]) + 'Lakh ' : '';
        str += (n[3] != 0) ? (a[Number(n[3])] || b[n[3][0]] + ' ' + a[n[3][1]]) + 'Thousand ' : '';
        str += (n[4] != 0) ? (a[Number(n[4])] || b[n[4][0]] + ' ' + a[n[4][1]]) + 'Hundred ' : '';
        str += (n[5] != 0) ? ((str != '') ? 'and ' : '') + (a[Number(n[5])] || b[n[5][0]] + ' ' + a[n[5][1]]) : '';
        return str + "Rupees Only"; }

    function filterTable(inputId, tableBodyId) {
        const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
        const filter = input.value.toLowerCase();
        const tbody = document.getElementById(tableBodyId);
        const rows = tbody.getElementsByTagName('tr');

        for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
            // Skip the "No data available" row if it exists
            if (rows[i].cells.length === 1 && rows[i].cells[0].colSpan > 1) continue;
            
            let rowText = rows[i].textContent || rows[i].innerText;
            if (rowText.toLowerCase().indexOf(filter) > -1) {
                rows[i].style.display = "";
            } else {
                rows[i].style.display = "none";
            }
        }
    }

        if(id === 'cashbook') renderCashBook();
        if(id === 'reports' && document.getElementById('rep-sec-fintb').style.display !== 'none') renderFinancials();
        if(id === 'pnl') renderPnL();
    }

    // ==========================================
// UNIFIED INVOICE FILTER ENGINE (Your Advanced Logic + Tabs)
// ==========================================

let currentGlobalInvType = 'All'; // Remembers which tab is currently selected

// 1. Handles clicks on the Nav Pills (Tax Invoice, Quotation, etc.)
function filterType(type, element) {
    currentGlobalInvType = type;
    
    // Highlight the correct tab
    document.querySelectorAll('#inv-tabs .nav-link').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
    if(element) {
        element.classList.add('active');
    } else {
        // Fallback for "Clear Filters"
        let firstTab = document.querySelector('#inv-tabs .nav-link');
        if(firstTab) firstTab.classList.add('active'); 
    }
    
    filterInvoices(); // Trigger the master filter
}
// ==========================================
// UNIFIED INVOICE FILTERING SYSTEM
// ==========================================

// 1. GLOBAL MEMORY FOR ACTIVE TAB
window.currentGlobalInvType = 'All';

// 2. THE TAB CLICK FUNCTION
function filterType(type, elem) {
    // Remove blue highlight from all tabs
    document.querySelectorAll('#inv-tabs .nav-link').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
    
    // Add highlight to clicked tab safely
    if(elem) elem.classList.add('active');
    
    // Save to global memory
    window.currentGlobalInvType = type;
    
    // Trigger the main filter
    filterInvoices();
}

// ==========================================
// FIXED INVOICE FILTER (Search + Tab + Pagination Friendly)
// ==========================================
function filterInvoices() {
    const start = document.getElementById('filt-start') ? document.getElementById('filt-start').value : "";
    const end = document.getElementById('filt-end') ? document.getElementById('filt-end').value : "";
    const client = (document.getElementById('filt-client') ? document.getElementById('filt-client').value : "").toLowerCase();
    const site = (document.getElementById('filt-site') ? document.getElementById('filt-site').value : "").toLowerCase();
    const statusFilter = (document.getElementById('filt-status') ? document.getElementById('filt-status').value : "").toLowerCase();

    const searchInput = (document.getElementById('search-inv') ? document.getElementById('search-inv').value : "").toLowerCase().trim();

    const tbody = document.getElementById('tbl-invoices');
    if (!tbody) return;

    const rows = tbody.getElementsByTagName('tr');

    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        const row = rows[i];
        if (row.cells.length < 5) continue;

        let rowText = row.textContent.toLowerCase();

        // 1. Tab Filter (Tax Invoice / Proforma / Quotation / Challan)
        let matchType = true;
        if (window.currentGlobalInvType && window.currentGlobalInvType !== 'All') {
            const typeCell = row.cells[0] ? row.cells[0].textContent.toLowerCase() : "";
            if (!typeCell.includes(window.currentGlobalInvType.toLowerCase())) {
                matchType = false;
            }
        }

        // 2. Date Range
        let matchDate = true;
        const rowDateStr = row.cells[1] ? row.cells[1].textContent.trim() : "";
        if (start && rowDateStr && rowDateStr < start) matchDate = false;
        if (end && rowDateStr && rowDateStr > end) matchDate = false;

        // 3. Client, Site, Status
        let matchClient = !client || rowText.includes(client);
        let matchSite = !site || rowText.includes(site);
        let matchStatus = true;
        if (statusFilter && statusFilter !== "") {
            const statusCell = row.cells[4] ? row.cells[4].textContent.toLowerCase() : "";
            matchStatus = statusCell.includes(statusFilter);
        }

        // 4. General Search
        let matchSearch = !searchInput || rowText.includes(searchInput);

        // Final Decision
        if (matchType && matchDate && matchClient && matchSite && matchStatus && matchSearch) {
            row.style.display = '';
        } else {
            row.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
}


// 3. Upgraded Clear Filters (Resets Dropdowns AND Search AND Tabs)
function clearInvFilters(){ 
    document.getElementById('filt-start').value = "";
    document.getElementById('filt-end').value = ""; 
    document.getElementById('filt-client').value = ""; 
    document.getElementById('filt-site').value = ""; 
    document.getElementById('filt-status').value = ""; 
    
    // Safely clear whichever search bar exists
    let oldSearch = document.getElementById("inv-search");
    if(oldSearch) oldSearch.value = ""; 
    
    let newSearch = document.getElementById("search-inv");
    if(newSearch) newSearch.value = "";
    
    // Reset the tabs back to 'All'
    filterType('All', null);
}

            if (elUnit && DATA.inputs.units) elUnit.innerHTML = DATA.inputs.units.map(function(u) { return '<option value="' + u + '">'; }).join('');
            
            let elHead = document.getElementById('list-head');
            if (elHead && DATA.inputs.heads) elHead.innerHTML = DATA.inputs.heads.map(function(h) { return '<option value="' + h + '">'; }).join('');
        }

        if (elCostHeads && DATA.costHeads) elCostHeads.innerHTML = DATA.costHeads.map(function(c) { return '<option value="' + c.name + '">'; }).join('');
    }

    function duplicateInvoice(i) { const inv = DATA.invoices[i]; initInvoice(); document.getElementById('i-type').value = inv.type; updateInvNum(); document.getElementById('i-date').valueAsDate = new Date();
        const clientRecord = DATA.clients.find(function(c) { return c.name === inv.company && (c.site || "Main Site") === (inv.siteName || "Main Site"); });
        if (clientRecord) { document.getElementById('i-filter-state').value = clientRecord.state; filterCompanies(); document.getElementById('i-company').value = inv.company; filterSites(); const ss = document.getElementById('i-site-select');
        for (let x = 0; x < ss.options.length; x++) { if (ss.options[x].text === (inv.siteName || "Main Site")) { ss.selectedIndex = x;
        break; } } fillCli(); } document.getElementById('i-from').value = inv.billFrom; document.getElementById('i-to').value = inv.billTo; document.getElementById('i-so').value = inv.soNumber; document.getElementById('i-veh').value = inv.vehicle; document.getElementById('i-ship').value = inv.ship;
        document.getElementById('i-desc').value = inv.work; document.getElementById('i-terms').value = inv.terms; document.getElementById('i-items').innerHTML = ""; 
        
        JSON.parse(inv.items).forEach(function(it) { const tr = '<tr><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d" value="' + (it.desc || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" value="' + (it.costHead || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" value="' + (it.sac || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" value="' + (it.uom || '') + '"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" value="' + (it.qty || '') + '" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" value="' + (it.rate || '') + '" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly value="' + (it.amount || '') + '"></td><td><button class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest(\'tr\').remove();calc()"></button></td></tr>'; document.getElementById('i-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr); });
        calc(); Toast.fire({icon:'info', title:'Invoice Duplicated'}); }
    function convertQuote(i) {
    const doc = DATA.invoices[i];
    const docNum = doc.invNum || doc[0]; 
    
    Swal.fire({
        title: 'Convert Document?',
        text: `This will prepare a new Tax Invoice from ${docNum}. It will NOT be locked until you click Save.`,
        icon: 'question',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#1a73e8',
        confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Convert'
    }).then((result) => {
        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            // 🚀 Instant UI generation (No dangerous backend lock needed here!)
            duplicateInvoice(i); 
            document.getElementById('i-type').value = "Tax Invoice"; 
            if(typeof updateInvNum === 'function') updateInvNum();
            document.getElementById('i-date').valueAsDate = new Date(); 
            
            // Stamp the old PI Number into the hidden field!
            let convertInput = document.getElementById('convertedFrom');
            if(convertInput) convertInput.value = docNum;
            
            Swal.fire('Draft Ready', "Data copied. Please save the new Tax Invoice to finalize the conversion.", 'success');
        }
    });
}

function convertToDC(i) {
    const doc = DATA.invoices[i];
    const docNum = doc.invNum || doc[0]; 
    
    Swal.fire({
        title: 'Convert to Delivery Challan?',
        text: `This will permanently lock ${docNum}.`,
        icon: 'question',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#ffc107',
        confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Convert'
    }).then((result) => {
        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            showLoad();
            const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(res => {
                    hideLoad();
                    const r = JSON.parse(res);
                    if(r.status === 'error') return Swal.fire('Error', r.message, 'error');
                    
                    duplicateInvoice(i); 
                    document.getElementById('i-type').value = "Delivery Challan"; 
                    if(typeof updateInvNum === 'function') updateInvNum();
                    document.getElementById('i-date').valueAsDate = new Date(); 
                    
                    Swal.fire('Draft Ready', "Data copied. Please save the Delivery Challan.", 'success');
                    silentSync(); 
                })
                .withFailureHandler(err => { hideLoad(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); })
                .lockDocument(token, docNum, "CONVERTED TO DC");
        }
    });
}
