    function addItem() { 
        const tr = '<tr><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" placeholder="Cost Head *"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" placeholder="Type SAC"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" placeholder="Unit"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly></td><td><button class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest(\'tr\').remove();calc()"></button></td></tr>';

    function updateInvNum() { 
        const t = document.getElementById('i-type').value;
        if (DATA.nextIds) { 
            if (t === "Tax Invoice") document.getElementById('i-no').value = DATA.nextIds.SD; 
            else if (t === "Proforma Invoice") document.getElementById('i-no').value = DATA.nextIds.PI;
            else if (t === "Quotation") document.getElementById('i-no').value = DATA.nextIds.QUO; 
            else if (t === "Delivery Challan") document.getElementById('i-no').value = DATA.nextIds.DC;
            else if (t === "Credit Note") document.getElementById('i-no').value = DATA.nextIds.CN;
        }  }
    // ==========================================
// PREMIUM CSV EXPORTER (Fixed Columns & Visibility)
// ==========================================
function exportToCSV(tid, fn) {
    let csv = [];
    const table = document.getElementById(tid);
    const rows = table.rows;
    let excludeColumnIndices = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        // 1. VISIBILITY CHECK: Skip this row if it is hidden by pagination or search filters
        if (window.getComputedStyle(rows[i]).display === 'none') {
            continue;
        }

        let row = [];
        let cols = rows[i].querySelectorAll("td, th");

        for (let j = 0; j < cols.length; j++) {
            // Clean the text to check it accurately
            let cellText = cols[j].innerText.trim().toUpperCase(); 

            // 2. HEADER CHECK: If this is the first row (Header), map out the columns to ban
            if (i === 0) {
                if (cellText === "ACT" || cellText === "ACTION" || cellText === "ACTIONS" || cellText === "RECEIPT") {
                    excludeColumnIndices.push(j); // Remember this column index to skip it forever
                    continue; 
                }
            } else {
                // 3. BODY CHECK: If this cell's column index is on the banned list, skip it!
                if (excludeColumnIndices.includes(j)) {
                    continue;
                }
            }

            // 4. DATA CLEANUP: Remove Rupees, format for CSV
            let val = cols[j].innerText.replace(/₹/g, '').replace(/"/g, '""').trim();
            row.push('"' + val + '"');
        }

        // Only add the row to the CSV if it actually has data
        if (row.length > 0) {
            csv.push(row.join(","));
        }
    }

    // 5. TRIGGER DOWNLOAD (Added BOM '%EF%BB%BF' so Excel reads special characters perfectly)
    let link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = "data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,%EF%BB%BF" + encodeURIComponent(csv.join("\n"));
    link.download = fn;
    link.click();
}
    function toggleHist() { const d = document.getElementById('hist-area'); d.style.display = d.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none'; }
    function toggleInvList() { const d = document.getElementById('inv-list-container'); d.style.display = d.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none'; }
    function sortT(n, tid) { var t, r, sw, i, x, y, sh; t = document.getElementById(tid); sw = true; while (sw) { sw = false; r = t.rows; for (i = 1; i < (r.length - 1); i++) { sh = false;
        x = r[i].getElementsByTagName("TD")[n]; y = r[i + 1].getElementsByTagName("TD")[n]; if (x.innerHTML.toLowerCase() > y.innerHTML.toLowerCase()) { sh = true; break;
        } } if (sh) { r[i].parentNode.insertBefore(r[i + 1], r[i]); sw = true;
        } } }
        
    function saveSysConfig(e) { 
        e.preventDefault(); 
        showLoad();
        
        try {
            const d = { 
                pin: document.getElementById('adm-pin').value, 
                user_pin: document.getElementById('adm-user-pin').value,
                folder: document.getElementById('adm-folder').value,

                signature: document.getElementById('adm-signature').value, 
                upi: document.getElementById('adm-upi').value, 
                // FIX: Updated to the correct HTML ID for the overdue box
                overdue: document.getElementById('setting-overdue').value, 
                freeze_days: document.getElementById('adm-freeze').value,
                useDirectKeys: false 
            };
            
            google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function() { 
                Toast.fire({icon:'success', title:'System Config Saved'}); 
                hideLoad(); 
                silentSync(); 
            })
            // Added failure handler so the spinner stops if the server crashes
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                hideLoad();
                Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error');
            })
            .saveSettings(sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'), JSON.stringify(d));
            
        } catch (err) {

            Swal.fire('Frontend Error', err.message, 'error');
        }
    }

    function shareWa() { 
        const pdf = document.getElementById('i-pdf-link').href;
        if(!pdf || pdf.includes('#')) return Swal.fire('Error', "Generate PDF first", 'warning'); 
        
        const clientName = document.getElementById('i-company').value;
        const invNo = document.getElementById('i-no').value;
        const amt = document.getElementById('i-total').innerText;
        
        // Find client phone number from DB
        const clientObj = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === clientName);
        let phone = clientObj ? clientObj.phone : "";
        
        let msg = `Hello ${clientName},\n\nYour document ${invNo} for ₹${amt} has been generated.\n\nYou can view and download it securely here: ${pdf}\n\nThank you,\nSai Durga`;
        
        let url = 'https://wa.me/';
        if(phone) {
            phone = phone.replace(/\D/g, ''); // Remove dashes/spaces
            if(phone.length === 10) phone = '91' + phone; // Add India code if missing
            url += phone;
        }
        
        url += '?text=' + encodeURIComponent(msg);
        window.open(url, '_blank'); 
    }
    function quickEmailPO(poNum) {
        const p = DATA.purchaseOrders.find(x => x.poNum === poNum);
        if(!p || !p.pdf || p.pdf.includes('#')) return Swal.fire('Error', "PDF not generated yet.", 'warning');
        const vendor = DATA.vendors ? DATA.vendors.find(v => v.name === p.vendor) : null;
        const email = vendor ? vendor.email : "";
        fireEmailComposerSwal(p.poNum, p.pdf, p.vendor, email);
    }

    function switchContactTab(t) { 
        document.querySelectorAll('.contact-section').forEach(function(d) { d.style.display = 'none'; });
        document.getElementById('contact-sec-' + t).style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelectorAll('#contacts-tabs .nav-link').forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); });
        event.target.classList.add('active');
    }

        document.querySelectorAll('.order-section').forEach(function(d) { d.style.display = 'none'; });
        document.getElementById('order-sec-' + t).style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelectorAll('#orders-tabs .nav-link').forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); });
        event.target.classList.add('active');
    }
    function switchMainRepTab(t) { 
        document.querySelectorAll('.report-section').forEach(function(d) { d.style.display = 'none'; });
        document.getElementById('rep-sec-' + t).style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelectorAll('#main-report-tabs .nav-link').forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); }); 
        
        if(['gst','gstr3b','liability','gsttracker','hsn','bulkupdate'].includes(t)) { 
            document.querySelector('#main-report-tabs .dropdown-toggle').classList.add('active');
        } else if(['fintb','finbs','finjournal'].includes(t)) { 
            document.getElementById('tab-core-fin').classList.add('active');
        } else { 
            if(event && event.target) event.target.classList.add('active'); 
        } 
        // Prevents Dropdown items (like P&L Analytics) from staying permanently blue
    document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
    }

        document.getElementById('gst-' + t).style.display = 'block'; document.querySelectorAll('.sub-nav .nav-link').forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); }); event.target.classList.add('active');
    }

    function executeLockBackend(m, action) {
        // 1. Grab the secure token!
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        if (!token) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Session Expired. Please log in again.', 'error');

        showLoad();
        google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(r) {
                hideLoad();
                const res = JSON.parse(r);
                if(res.status === 'success') {
                    Swal.fire('Success', res.message, 'success');
                    refreshGSTLocks();
                } else {
                    Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
                }
            })
            // 2. Add Failure Handler to STOP infinite loading if the server drops
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                hideLoad();
                Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || err, 'error');
            })
            .setGSTLock(token, m, action); // <-- Passing token here!
    }

    function refreshGSTLocks() {
        const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-gst-locks');
        if (tb) tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-3"><div class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm text-primary me-2"></div>Loading statuses...</td></tr>';

        google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
                const response = JSON.parse(res);
                if (response.status === "error") {
                    if (tb) tb.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-danger py-3"><i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill me-2"></i>${response.message}</td></tr>`;
                    return Swal.fire('Error', response.message, 'error');
                }

                const data = response.data;
                if (data.length === 0) {
                    if (tb) tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4"><i class="bi bi-inbox fs-4 d-block mb-2"></i>No months have been locked or unlocked yet.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }

                if (tb) tb.innerHTML = ""; 
                data.forEach(r => {
                    const statusBadge = String(r[1]).toUpperCase() === "TRUE" 
                        ? '<span class="badge bg-danger rounded-pill shadow-sm px-3">LOCKED</span>' 
                        : '<span class="badge bg-success rounded-pill shadow-sm px-3">OPEN</span>';
                    if (tb) tb.innerHTML += `<tr><td class="fw-bold">${r[0]}</td><td>${statusBadge}</td><td>${r[2] || '-'}</td><td class="text-muted small">${r[3] || '-'}</td></tr>`;
                });
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                if (tb) tb.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-danger py-3">Server Connection Failed.</td></tr>`;
            })
            .getGSTLocks();
    }

    function setGSTLockStatus(action) {
        const m = document.getElementById('adm-unified-month').value;
        if(!m) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Select a Month first', 'error');
        
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
            hideLoad();
            const r = JSON.parse(res);
            Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: r.message});
            refreshGSTLocks();
        }).setGSTLock(m, action);
    }

function renderCashBook() {
    if (!DATA) return;
    
    let obAmt = 0;
    let obDate = '2024-04-01';
    
    // Pull straight from the source of truth, ignoring the broken variable
    if(DATA.config) {
        obAmt = DATA.config.CASHBOOK_OB !== undefined ? parseFloat(DATA.config.CASHBOOK_OB) : 0;
        obDate = DATA.config.CASHBOOK_DATE || '2024-04-01';
    }
    
    let obInput = document.getElementById('cb-ob');
    let dateInput = document.getElementById('cb-date');
    if(obInput && dateInput) {
        obInput.value = obAmt;
        dateInput.value = obDate;
    }
    
    generateCashBookTable();
}

// ==========================================
// CASHBOOK TABLE GENERATOR (DECOUPLED & CRASH-PROOF)
// ==========================================
function generateCashBookTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('tbl-cashbook-body');
  if (!tbody) return;

  try {
    // 🚀 THE FIX: Completely bypass the broken 'cashbookConfig' variable!
    let startingBal = 0;
    let startingDate = '2024-04-01';

    if (typeof DATA !== 'undefined' && DATA && DATA.config) {
        startingBal = parseFloat(DATA.config.CASHBOOK_OB) || 0;
        startingDate = DATA.config.CASHBOOK_DATE || '2024-04-01';
    }

    // Live UI Sync: If you type a new value into the boxes, use that instead!
    let obInput = document.getElementById('cb-ob');
    let dateInput = document.getElementById('cb-date');
    if (obInput && obInput.value) startingBal = parseFloat(obInput.value) || 0;
    if (dateInput && dateInput.value) startingDate = dateInput.value;

    let html = '';
    let runningBal = startingBal;

    let obCleanDate = String(startingDate).trim();
    if (obCleanDate.includes('T')) obCleanDate = obCleanDate.split('T')[0];

    html += `
      <tr class="table-secondary fw-bold">
        <td class="py-2">${obCleanDate || "-"}</td>
        <td class="py-2">-</td>
        <td class="py-2">Opening Balance</td>
        <td class="text-end text-success py-2">-</td>
        <td class="text-end text-danger py-2">-</td>
        <td class="text-end text-dark bg-light border-start py-2">₹${runningBal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
        <td class="text-center py-2">-</td>
      </tr>
    `;

    let validTxns = [];
    if (typeof DATA !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(DATA.payments)) {
        DATA.payments.forEach((p, idx) => {
            let pCleanDate = String(p.date || "").trim();

            if (pCleanDate.includes('T')) {
                pCleanDate = pCleanDate.split('T')[0];
            } else if (pCleanDate !== "") {
                if (pCleanDate.includes('-') && pCleanDate.split('-')[0].length === 2) {
                    let parts = pCleanDate.split('-');
                    pCleanDate = `${parts[2]}-${parts[1]}-${parts[0]}`; 
                } else {
                    const dObj = new Date(p.date);
                    if (!isNaN(dObj.getTime())) {
                        const yyyy = dObj.getFullYear();
                        const mm = String(dObj.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                        const dd = String(dObj.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
                        pCleanDate = `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!pCleanDate) pCleanDate = "2000-01-01";

            if (pCleanDate >= obCleanDate) {
                validTxns.push({ ...p, cleanDate: pCleanDate, realArrayIndex: idx });
            }
        });
    }

    // (Existing Code)
    validTxns.sort((a, b) => String(a.cleanDate).localeCompare(String(b.cleanDate)));

    // 🚀 PERFORMANCE FIX: Limit to the most recent 150 transactions to prevent UI lag
    const renderLimit = 150;
    if (validTxns.length > renderLimit) {
        validTxns = validTxns.slice(-renderLimit);
    }

    const safeEscape = (str) => {
        let s = String(str || "");
        return typeof escapeHtml === "function" ? escapeHtml(s) : s;
    };
    // (Existing Code continues...)

    validTxns.forEach(p => {
        let amt = parseFloat(p.amount) || 0;
        let moneyIn = 0;
        let moneyOut = 0;

        let pType = String(p.type || "");

        if (pType === "GST Payment" || pType === "Vendor Payment" || pType === "Expense") {
            moneyOut = Math.abs(amt);
        } else {
            if (amt > 0) moneyIn = amt;
            else moneyOut = Math.abs(amt);
        }

        runningBal += (moneyIn - moneyOut);

        const party = safeEscape(p.client || "GST / Expense");
        const type = safeEscape(p.type || "Transaction");
        const safeRef = safeEscape(p.ref || "");

        const refHtml = safeRef && safeRef !== "-" ? `<br><small class="font-monospace text-muted px-1 bg-white border rounded">Ref: ${safeRef}</small>` : "";
        const badgeColor = moneyIn > 0 ? 'bg-success' : 'bg-danger';

        const delBtn = (typeof userRole !== 'undefined' && userRole === 'admin') ? 
            `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-light border text-primary px-2 py-1 shadow-sm" onclick="nav('payments'); editPayment(${p.realArrayIndex});" title="Edit Transaction"><i class="bi bi-pencil-fill"></i></button>` : '';

        const displayMoneyIn = moneyIn > 0 ? '+₹' + moneyIn.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) : '-';
        const displayMoneyOut = moneyOut > 0 ? '₹' + moneyOut.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) : '-';

        html += `
          <tr>
            <td class="py-2">${p.cleanDate}</td>
            <td class="py-2 fw-bold text-dark">${party}${refHtml}</td>
            <td class="py-2"><span class="badge ${badgeColor} bg-opacity-10 text-dark border rounded-pill">${type}</span></td>
            <td class="py-2 text-end text-success fw-bold">${displayMoneyIn}</td>
            <td class="py-2 text-end text-danger fw-bold">${displayMoneyOut}</td>
            <td class="py-2 text-end fw-bold ${runningBal < 0 ? 'bg-danger bg-opacity-10 text-danger' : 'bg-success bg-opacity-10 text-success'} border-start">₹${runningBal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
            <td class="py-2 text-center">${delBtn}</td>
          </tr>
        `;
    });

    tbody.innerHTML = html;

    const cbCard = document.getElementById('cb-current-bal');
    if(cbCard) {
        cbCard.innerText = '₹' + runningBal.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
        cbCard.className = runningBal < 0 ? "mb-0 fw-bold fs-4 text-danger" : "mb-0 fw-bold fs-4 text-primary";
    }

  } catch (err) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-danger p-3 fw-bold bg-danger bg-opacity-10">
      <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill me-2"></i> Cashbook Data Error: ${err.message}
    </td></tr>`;
    console.error("Cashbook Crash Details:", err);
  }
}
    function runHealthCheck() {

  function runValidationWizardUnified() {
        const m = document.getElementById('adm-unified-month').value;
        if(!m) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Please select a Target Month first.', 'warning');
        
        showLoad();
        Promise.all([
            new Promise((res) => google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res).runMonthEndChecklist(m)),
            new Promise((res) => google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res).getGSTReconciliationReport(m))
        ]).then(responses => {
            hideLoad();
            const chk = JSON.parse(responses[0]);
            const recon = JSON.parse(responses[1]);
            const resBox = document.getElementById('unified-wizard-results');
            resBox.style.display = 'block';
            
            let html = `<h6 class="fw-bold text-${chk.status === 'READY' ? 'success' : 'danger'}">System Integrity: ${chk.status}</h6>`;
            
            if(chk.issues && chk.issues.length > 0) {
                html += `<ul class="text-danger ps-3 mb-3">`;
                chk.issues.forEach(i => html += `<li>${i}</li>`);
                html += `</ul>`;
            } else {
                html += `<p class="text-success fw-bold"><i class="bi bi-check-circle"></i> Journal and Invoice Integrity Passed perfectly.</p>`;
            }
            
            html += `<hr><h6 class="fw-bold text-dark">GST Reconciliation</h6>
                     <table class="table table-sm table-borderless mb-0">
                         <tr><td>Output GST (Sales Liability):</td><td class="text-end">₹${recon.OutputGST.toFixed(2)}</td></tr>
                         <tr><td>ITC (Purchases):</td><td class="text-end text-success">- ₹${recon.ITCAvailable.toFixed(2)}</td></tr>
                         <tr class="fw-bold border-top"><td>Net Payable to Govt:</td><td class="text-end text-warning">₹${recon.NetGSTLiability.toFixed(2)}</td></tr>
                         <tr class="fw-bold"><td>GST Actually Paid (Bank):</td><td class="text-end ${recon.MismatchAlert ? 'text-danger' : 'text-success'}">₹${recon.GSTPaidToGovt.toFixed(2)}</td></tr>
                     </table>`;
                     
            if(recon.MismatchAlert) {
                html += `<div class="alert alert-danger py-1 mt-2 mb-0"><i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle"></i> Alert: Actual Tax Paid does not match your Net Liability!</div>`;
            }
            resBox.innerHTML = html;
        });
    }

    function setGSTLockStatusUnified(action) {
        const m = document.getElementById('adm-unified-month').value;
        if(!m) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Select a Target Month first.', 'warning');
        
        const resultsBox = document.getElementById('unified-wizard-results');
        
        // Smart UX: Warn them if they haven't scanned the month yet
        if(action === 'LOCK' && resultsBox.style.display === 'none') {
            return Swal.fire({
                title: 'Skip Integrity Scan?',
                text: 'You have not run the Pre-Close Scan. Are you sure you want to blindly lock this financial period?',
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Lock Blindly'
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) executeLockBackend(m, action);
            });
        }
        
        executeLockBackend(m, action);
    }

    function executeLockBackend(m, action) {
        // 1. Grab the secure token!
        const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
        if (!token) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Session Expired. Please log in again.', 'error');

        showLoad();
        google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(r) {
                hideLoad();
                const res = JSON.parse(r);
                if(res.status === 'success') {
                    Swal.fire('Success', res.message, 'success');
                    refreshGSTLocks();
                } else {
                    Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
                }
            })
            // 2. Add Failure Handler to STOP infinite loading if the server drops
            .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                hideLoad();
                Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || err, 'error');
            })
            .setGSTLock(token, m, action); // <-- Passing token here!
    }
    function activateAutomations() {
        showLoad();
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(resStr => {
            hideLoad();
            const res = JSON.parse(resStr);

    }
    function filterPaymentHistory() {
        const start = document.getElementById('pay-filt-start').value;
        const end = document.getElementById('pay-filt-end').value;
        const rows = document.querySelectorAll("#tbl-payments tr");
        
        rows.forEach(row => {
            if (row.cells.length <= 1) return; // Skip empty rows
            const dateStr = row.cells[0].innerText.trim();
            let show = true;
            if (start && dateStr < start) show = false;
            if (end && dateStr > end) show = false;
            row.style.display = show ? "" : "none";
        });
    }
    
    // --- YEAR END ARCHIVAL UI ---
    function promptYearEndRollover() {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Year-End Archival Engine',
            html: '<p class="small text-muted mb-3">This engine will safely move <b>FULLY PAID</b> invoices created on or before the selected date into the Archive database. Unpaid invoices will safely remain active.</p>' +
                  '<input type="date" id="archive-cutoff" class="form-control">',
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
            confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-archive me-1"></i> Execute Archival',
            preConfirm: () => {
                const dt = document.getElementById('archive-cutoff').value;
                if (!dt) Swal.showValidationMessage('Please select a cutoff date');
                return dt;
            }
        }).then((result) => {
            if (result.isConfirmed) {
                showLoad();
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(resStr => {
                    hideLoad();
                    const res = JSON.parse(resStr);
                    if (res.status === 'success') {
                        Swal.fire('Archived!', res.message, 'success').then(() => loadData());
                    } else {
                        Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
                    }
                }).executeYearEndRollover(result.value);
            }
        });
    }
    function autoFillStateFromGST(gstinInputId, stateDropdownId) {
        const gstin = document.getElementById(gstinInputId).value.toUpperCase();
        if(gstin.length >= 2) {
            const code = gstin.substring(0, 2);
            const stateMap = {
                "37": "Andhra Pradesh", "36": "Telangana", "29": "Karnataka", 
                "33": "Tamil Nadu", "27": "Maharashtra", "07": "Delhi", "24": "Gujarat","09": "Uttar Pradesh","06": "Haryana"
            };
            if(stateMap[code]) {
                document.getElementById(stateDropdownId).value = stateMap[code];
            }
        }
    }
        // --- WORK ORDER AUTO-FILL ENGINE (ULTIMATE EDITION) ---
    function autoFillFromWO() {
        const woNum = document.getElementById('i-so').value.trim().toUpperCase();
        if(!woNum || !DATA.workOrders) return;
        
        const wo = DATA.workOrders.find(w => String(w.woNo).toUpperCase() === woNum);
        if(wo) {
            const clientRecord = DATA.clients.find(c => c.name === wo.client && (c.site || "Main Site") === (wo.site || "Main Site"));
            if(clientRecord) {
                document.getElementById('i-filter-state').value = clientRecord.state;
                filterCompanies();
                document.getElementById('i-company').value = clientRecord.name;
                filterSites();
                
                const ss = document.getElementById('i-site-select');
                for(let i=0; i<ss.options.length; i++) {
                    if(ss.options[i].text === (clientRecord.site || "Main Site")) { 
                        ss.selectedIndex = i; break; 
                    }
                }
                fillCli(); 
            }
            
            document.getElementById('i-desc').value = wo.desc;

            // SMART BALANCE CALCULATOR: Subtracts past billed quantities!
            if(wo.items && wo.items.length > 5) {
                try {
                    const woItems = JSON.parse(wo.items);
                    
                    // 1. Calculate how much has already been billed for each item
                    let billedQtyMap = {}; 
                    (DATA.invoices || []).forEach(inv => {
                        if (String(inv.soNumber).trim().toUpperCase() === woNum && inv.status !== 'Cancelled') {
                            try {
                                let invItems = JSON.parse(inv.items);
                                invItems.forEach(it => {
                                    let descKey = String(it.desc || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                                    billedQtyMap[descKey] = (billedQtyMap[descKey] || 0) + (parseFloat(it.qty) || 0);
                                });
                            } catch(e){}
                        }
                    });

                    document.getElementById('i-items').innerHTML = ""; // Clears default blank row
                    
                    // 2. Draw only the remaining balances!
                    let itemsAdded = 0;
                    woItems.forEach(function(it) { 
                        let descKey = String(it.desc || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                        let originalQty = parseFloat(it.qty) || 0;
                        let pastBilled = billedQtyMap[descKey] || 0;
                        let balanceQty = originalQty - pastBilled;
                        
                        // We only add the item to the new invoice if there is a balance left to bill!
                        if (balanceQty > 0) {
                            itemsAdded++;
                            const amount = (balanceQty * (parseFloat(it.rate) || 0)).toFixed(2);
                            const tr = '<tr><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d" value="' + (it.desc || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" value="' + (it.costHead || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" value="' + (it.sac || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" value="' + (it.uom || '') + '"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" value="' + balanceQty + '" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" value="' + (it.rate || '') + '" oninput="calc()"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly value="' + amount + '"></td><td><button type="button" class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest(\'tr\').remove();calc()"></button></td></tr>'; 
                            document.getElementById('i-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr); 
                        }
                    });
                    // ENTERPRISE FIX: Trigger GST and Total Calculation automatically
                    setTimeout(() => {
                    const rateEl = document.getElementById('inv-gst-rate') || document.getElementById('i-gstrate');
                    if(rateEl && (!rateEl.value || rateEl.value === "")) rateEl.value = "18"; 
                    if(typeof calcTotals === 'function') calcTotals();
                    if(typeof calc === 'function') calc();
                    }, 300);
                    calc(); // Instantly sums up all fetched items
                    
                    if (itemsAdded === 0) {
                        Swal.fire('Fully Billed', 'This Work Order has already been billed for its full quantity!', 'info');
                    } else {
                        Toast.fire({icon: 'success', title: 'Balance Quantities Synced!'});
                    }
                    
                } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
            }
        }
    }
    function filterWOSites() { 
        const cp = document.getElementById('wo-client').value; const ss = document.getElementById('wo-site');
        ss.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Site...</option>'; if (!cp) return; 
        DATA.clients.filter(c => c.name === cp).forEach(c => ss.add(new Option(c.site || "Main Site", c.site || "Main Site")));
    }
    function initWO() {
        showModal('modal-wo'); document.getElementById('form-wo').reset(); document.getElementById('wo-items').innerHTML = "";
        let maxWO = 0;
            if(DATA.workOrders) {
                DATA.workOrders.forEach(w => {
                    let m = String(w.woNo).match(/WO\/25-26\/(\d+)/);
                    if(m) maxWO = Math.max(maxWO, parseInt(m[1], 10));
                });
            }
            document.getElementById('wo-no').value = "WO/25-26/" + String(maxWO + 1).padStart(3, '0');
        document.getElementById('wo-start').valueAsDate = new Date();
        document.getElementById('wo-invLink').value = "";
        const cs = document.getElementById('wo-client'); cs.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Client...</option>';
        [...new Set((DATA.clients||[]).map(c => c.name))].sort().forEach(c => cs.add(new Option(c, c)));
        addWOItem();
    }
    function addWOItem() {
        const tr = '<tr><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" oninput="calcWO()"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" oninput="calcWO()"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly></td><td><button type="button" class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest(\'tr\').remove();calcWO()"></button></td></tr>';
        document.getElementById('wo-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr);
    }
    function calcWO() {
        let t = 0;
        document.querySelectorAll('#wo-items tr').forEach(function(r) { 
            const q = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.q').value) || 0; const rt = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.r').value) || 0; 
            const a = (q * rt).toFixed(2); r.querySelector('.a').value = a; t += parseFloat(a); 
        });
        document.getElementById('wo-total').innerText = t.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
    }
    // ==========================================
    // BULLETPROOF WORK ORDER SAVER
    // ==========================================
    function submitWO(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        showLoad(); // Spinner Starts

        try {
            // 1. Grab the Security Token
            const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
            if (!token) throw new Error("Security Token missing. Please log out and log back in.");
            
            // 2. Safely read the items table (ignores missing columns)
            const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('#wo-items tr')].map(tr => ({
                desc: tr.querySelector('.d') ? tr.querySelector('.d').value : "",
                costHead: tr.querySelector('.c') ? tr.querySelector('.c').value : "",
                sac: tr.querySelector('.s') ? tr.querySelector('.s').value : "",
                uom: tr.querySelector('.u') ? tr.querySelector('.u').value : "",
                qty: tr.querySelector('.q') ? tr.querySelector('.q').value : 0,
                rate: tr.querySelector('.r') ? tr.querySelector('.r').value : 0,
                amount: tr.querySelector('.a') ? tr.querySelector('.a').value : 0
            }));

            // 3. Build Payload
            const payload = {
                woNo: document.getElementById('wo-no').value,
                client: document.getElementById('wo-client').value,
                site: document.getElementById('wo-site').value,
                desc: document.getElementById('wo-desc').value,
                staff: document.getElementById('wo-staff').value,
                start: document.getElementById('wo-start').value,
                end: document.getElementById('wo-end').value,
                status: document.getElementById('wo-status').value,
                items: JSON.stringify(items)
            };

            // 4. Send to Backend safely
            google.script.run
                  .withSuccessHandler(res => {
                      hideLoad(); 
                      const r = JSON.parse(res);
                      if(r.status === 'success') {
                          Swal.fire('Saved!', 'Work Order Saved Successfully.', 'success');
                          closeModal('modal-wo');
                          silentSync();                   // ← Changed to silentSync
                      } else {
                          Swal.fire('Backend Error', r.message, 'error');
                      }
                  })
                .withFailureHandler(err => {
                    hideLoad(); // Spinner Stops on Google Server Error
                    Swal.fire('Connection Error', err.message, 'error');
                })
                .saveWO(token, JSON.stringify(payload)); // <-- Calls the backend securely
                
        } catch(err) {
            hideLoad(); // Spinner Stops on Local Browser Error
            Swal.fire('Form Error', err.message, 'error');
        }
    }
    function editWO(woNo) {
        const wo = DATA.workOrders.find(w => w.woNo === woNo); if(!wo) return;
        initWO(); document.getElementById('wo-no').value = wo.woNo; document.getElementById('wo-client').value = wo.client; filterWOSites(); document.getElementById('wo-site').value = wo.site;
        document.getElementById('wo-start').value = wo.start; document.getElementById('wo-end').value = wo.end; document.getElementById('wo-desc').value = wo.desc; document.getElementById('wo-staff').value = wo.staff; document.getElementById('wo-status').value = wo.status; document.getElementById('wo-invLink').value = wo.invLink || "";
        document.getElementById('wo-items').innerHTML = "";
        try { JSON.parse(wo.items).forEach(function(it) {
            const tr = '<tr><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm d" value="' + (it.desc || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm c" list="list-costheads" value="' + (it.costHead || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm s" list="list-sac" value="' + (it.sac || '') + '"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm u" list="list-unit" value="' + (it.uom || '') + '"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm q" value="' + (it.qty || '') + '" oninput="calcWO()"></td><td><input type="number" step="any" class="form-control form-control-sm r" value="' + (it.rate || '') + '" oninput="calcWO()"></td><td><input class="form-control form-control-sm a" readonly value="' + (it.amount || '') + '"></td><td><button type="button" class="btn-close" onclick="this.closest(\'tr\').remove();calcWO()"></button></td></tr>'; 
            document.getElementById('wo-items').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', tr); 
        }); } catch(e){} calcWO();
    }

    // ==========================================
    // WORK ORDER -> INVOICE CONVERTER (TIMED FIX)
    // ==========================================
    function convertWOtoInvoice(woNo) {
        // 1. Navigate to the Finance Tab
        try { nav('finance'); } catch(e) {}
        try { switchFinanceTab('invoices'); } catch(e) {}

        // 2. Safely call the exact initialization function to open the modal
        if (typeof initInv === 'function') {
            initInv();
        } else if (typeof initInvoice === 'function') {
            initInvoice();
        } else {
            showModal('modal-inv');
            try { document.getElementById('form-inv').reset(); } catch(e) {}
        }

        // 3. WAIT 400ms for the modal to fully open and load the new Invoice Number, THEN inject data
        setTimeout(() => {
            const soInput = document.getElementById('i-so') || document.getElementById('inv-so');
            if (soInput) {
                soInput.value = woNo;
                autoFillFromWO(); 
            } else {
                console.error("Work Order input field not found on the form.");
            }
        }, 400); 
    }    
    function printCleanLedger() {
    const party = document.getElementById('led-sum-party') ? document.getElementById('led-sum-party').innerText : "Ledger Statement";
    const closeBal = document.getElementById('led-sum-close') ? document.getElementById('led-sum-close').innerText : "";
    const tableHtml = document.getElementById('tbl-ledger').outerHTML;
    
    // 1. Remove any old hidden print frames
    let oldFrame = document.getElementById('hidden-print-frame');
    if (oldFrame) oldFrame.remove();

    // 2. Create the invisible iframe
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.id = 'hidden-print-frame';
    iframe.style.position = 'absolute';
    iframe.style.width = '0px';
    iframe.style.height = '0px';
    iframe.style.border = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(iframe);

    // Get current date for the timestamp
    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });

    // 3. Write the Premium Ledger into the hidden frame
    const doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
    doc.open();
    doc.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html>
        <head>
            <title>Ledger - ${party}</title>
            <style>
                /* Premium Print Typography */
                body { 
                    font-family: 'Helvetica Neue', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; 
                    padding: 0; 
                    color: #111; 
                    font-size: 12px;
                }
                
                /* Page Setup to prevent cut-off rows */
                @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 15mm; }
                
                /* Header Styling */
                .header-container { 
                    text-align: center; 
                    border-bottom: 2px solid #222; 
                    padding-bottom: 15px; 
                    margin-bottom: 20px; 
                }
                .company-name { font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 5px 0; letter-spacing: 1px; }
                .report-title { font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #555; text-transform: uppercase; margin: 0; }
                
                /* Details Box */
                .details-box {
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    margin-bottom: 20px;
                    background-color: #f9f9f9;
                    padding: 10px 15px;
                    border: 1px solid #ddd;
                    border-radius: 4px;
                }
                .details-box h3, .details-box h4 { margin: 0; font-size: 14px; }
                .timestamp { font-size: 11px; color: #666; }

                /* Table Styling */
                table { 
                    width: 100%; 
                    border-collapse: collapse; 
                    margin-top: 10px; 
                }
                th, td { 
                    border: 1px solid #ddd; 
                    padding: 8px 10px; 
                }
                th { 
                    background-color: #f4f4f4 !important; /* Force gray background on print */
                    -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
                    font-weight: 600;
                    text-align: left;
                    color: #333;
                }
                
                /* Prevent rows from splitting across pages */
                tr { page-break-inside: avoid; }
                
                /* Alignments & Colors */
                .text-end { text-align: right; }
                .text-center { text-align: center; }
                .fw-bold { font-weight: bold; }
                .text-danger { color: #d32f2f !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }
                .text-success { color: #2e7d32 !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }

                /* HIDE UI ELEMENTS ON PRINT */
                button, .btn, .action-col, td:last-child th:last-child { 
                    display: none !important; 
                }
                
                /* Footer */
                .footer {
                    margin-top: 30px;
                    text-align: center;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    color: #777;
                    border-top: 1px solid #eee;
                    padding-top: 10px;
                }
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <div class="header-container">
                <h1 class="company-name">SAI DURGA</h1>
                <h2 class="report-title">Statement of Account</h2>
            </div>

            <div class="details-box">
                <div>
                    <h3 style="color:#333;">Party: <span style="color:#000;">${party}</span></h3>
                    <div class="timestamp">Generated on: ${today}</div>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: right;">
                    <h4 style="color:#555;">Closing Balance</h4>
                    <h3 style="font-size: 18px; color: #111;">${closeBal}</h3>
                </div>
            </div>

            ${tableHtml}
            
            <div class="footer">
                This is a computer-generated document from SD Dynamics. No signature is required.
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
    `);
    doc.close();

    // 4. Give the browser time to render, then print
    setTimeout(function() {
        iframe.contentWindow.focus();
        iframe.contentWindow.print();
    }, 500);
}

    function promptCashBookEmail() {
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Email Cash Book', input: 'email', inputPlaceholder: 'accountant@client.com', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonText: 'Send Report' }).then((res) => {
            if(res.isConfirmed && res.value) {
                showLoad();
                const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
                const cbData = [...document.querySelectorAll('#tbl-cashbook-main tbody tr')].map(tr => {
                    const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td'); if(tds.length < 6) return null;
                    return { date: tds[0].innerText, ref: tds[1].innerText, type: tds[2].innerText, in: tds[3].innerText, out: tds[4].innerText, bal: tds[5].innerText };
                }).filter(x => x !== null);
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => { hideLoad(); const parsed = JSON.parse(r); if(parsed.status === 'success') Swal.fire('Sent!', 'Emailed successfully.', 'success'); else Swal.fire('Error', parsed.message, 'error'); }).withFailureHandler(err => { hideLoad(); Swal.fire('Error', err.message, 'error'); }).sendCashBookPDF(token, res.value, JSON.stringify(cbData));
            }
        });
    }

    // FIX: SECURE EMAIL STATEMENT GENERATOR
    // ==========================================
    function emailClientStatement() {
        const client = document.getElementById('led-client').value;
        const site = document.getElementById('led-site').value;
        const closeBal = document.getElementById('led-sum-close') ? document.getElementById('led-sum-close').innerText : "";
        if (!client) return Swal.fire('Info', "Please select a client and load the ledger first.", 'info');
        const partyName = client + (site ? " (" + site + ")" : "");
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Email Statement', input: 'email', inputPlaceholder: 'client@company.com', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonText: 'Send Statement' }).then((res) => {
            if(res.isConfirmed && res.value) {
                showLoad();
                const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
                const ledgerData = [...document.querySelectorAll('#tbl-ledger tbody tr')].map(tr => { const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td'); if (tds.length < 5) return null; return { date: tds[0].innerText, ref: tds[1].innerText, type: "-", dr: tds[2].innerText, cr: tds[3].innerText, bal: tds[4].innerText }; }).filter(item => item !== null);
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r => { hideLoad(); const parsed = JSON.parse(r); if(parsed.status === 'success') Swal.fire('Sent!', 'PDF Statement generated and emailed.', 'success'); else Swal.fire('Error', parsed.message, 'error'); }).sendStatementPDF(token, partyName, res.value, JSON.stringify(ledgerData), closeBal);
            }
        });
    }
    function triggerOverdueEmails() {
    showLoad(); // Turn on spinner
    
    // 1. Fetch the Security Token
    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
    
    google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(r => { 
            hideLoad(); 
            Swal.fire("AI Dispatch Result", r, "info"); 
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => { 
            hideLoad(); 
            // 2. Safe Error Extraction
            const errorMsg = e.message ? e.message : e; 
            Swal.fire("Error", errorMsg, "error"); 
        })
        // 3. Pass the token FIRST!
        .checkAndSendOverdueEmails(token); 
}
    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // GSTR-2A RECONCILIATION CONTROLLERS
    // ----------------------------------------------------
    function renderReconTable() {
        const tb = document.getElementById('tbl-recon-body');
        if (!tb) return;
        tb.innerHTML = '';
        
        if (!DATA.gstr2a || DATA.gstr2a.length === 0) {
            tb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center text-muted py-4">No GSTR-2A data uploaded yet.</td></tr>';
            return;
        }

        // SMART FEATURE: Check Purchases to determine if 2A record is matched
        const cleanInv = (inv) => String(inv).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase().trim();
        let matchedSet = new Set();
        
        if(DATA.purchases) {
            DATA.purchases.forEach(p => {
                let pStatus = String(p.status2A || p.status || "").toUpperCase();
                
                if(pStatus === "MATCHED") {
                    let pGstin = String(p.gstin || "").trim().toUpperCase();
                    let pInv = cleanInv(p.invNo || "");
                    if (pGstin && pInv) {
                        matchedSet.add(pGstin + "_" + pInv);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
        
        let html = '';
        [...DATA.gstr2a].reverse().forEach(r => {
            let lookupKey = String(r.gstin).trim().toUpperCase() + "_" + cleanInv(r.invNo);
            let isMatched = matchedSet.has(lookupKey);
            let statusBadge = isMatched ? '<span class="badge bg-success"><i class="bi bi-check-circle me-1"></i>Matched</span>' : '<span class="badge bg-warning text-dark"><i class="bi bi-hourglass-split me-1"></i>Pending</span>';

            // 🛡️ XSS SECURITY ARMOR: Sanitize all text fields before rendering
            const safeMonth = escapeHtml(r.month || '-');
            const safeDate = escapeHtml(r.date || '-');
            const safeGstin = escapeHtml(r.gstin || '-');
            const safeName = escapeHtml(r.name || '-');
            const safeInvNo = escapeHtml(r.invNo || '-');

            html += `<tr>
                <td class="fw-bold">${safeMonth}</td>
                <td>${safeDate}</td>
                <td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">${safeGstin}</span></td>
                <td class="text-truncate" style="max-width: 200px;" title="${safeName}">${safeName}</td>
                <td class="fw-bold text-primary">${safeInvNo}</td>
                <td class="text-end">₹${parseFloat(r.taxable || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="text-end text-danger fw-bold">₹${parseFloat(r.tax || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2})}</td>
                <td class="text-center">${statusBadge}</td>
            </tr>`;
        });
        tb.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function triggerReconEngine() {
        Swal.fire({
            title: '<i class="bi bi-magic text-primary fs-1"></i><br>Run Auto-Match',
            html: 'Which month would you like to reconcile against your Purchases ledger?',
            input: 'month',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Initialize Engine',
            confirmButtonColor: '#1a73e8'
        }).then((res) => {
            if (res.isConfirmed && res.value) {
                showLoad();
                const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken'); // <--- GRAB TOKEN
                
                google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(r => {
                    hideLoad();
                    const parsed = JSON.parse(r);
                    if (parsed.status === 'success') {
                        Swal.fire('Reconciliation Complete', parsed.message, 'success');
                        silentSync(); 
                    } else {
                        Swal.fire('Engine Error', parsed.message, 'error');
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(err => { // <--- ADD CRASH NET
                    hideLoad();
                    Swal.fire('Server Timeout', 'The reconciliation engine timed out, but data is safe. Please try again.', 'warning');
                })
                .runAutoReconciliation(token, res.value); // <--- SEND TOKEN HERE
            }
        });
    }

// CASHBOOK & DAY BOOK CONTROLLERS

function switchCashbookTab(tabName) {
    // Hide both tabs
    document.getElementById('cb-tab-ledger').classList.add('d-none');
    document.getElementById('cb-tab-daybook').classList.add('d-none');
    
    // Reset button styles to outline
    document.getElementById('btn-tab-ledger').className = 'btn btn-outline-primary fw-bold px-4 shadow-sm';
    document.getElementById('btn-tab-daybook').className = 'btn btn-outline-primary fw-bold px-4 shadow-sm';
    
    // Show the requested tab and make its button solid blue
    if(tabName === 'ledger') {
        document.getElementById('cb-tab-ledger').classList.remove('d-none');
        document.getElementById('btn-tab-ledger').className = 'btn btn-primary active fw-bold px-4 shadow-sm';
        
    } else if(tabName === 'daybook') {
        document.getElementById('cb-tab-daybook').classList.remove('d-none');
        document.getElementById('btn-tab-daybook').className = 'btn btn-primary active fw-bold px-4 shadow-sm';
        
        // Auto-set today's date if it's empty
        if(!document.getElementById('daybook-date').value) {
            document.getElementById('daybook-date').value = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA');
        }
        generateDayBook(); // Fetch the data
    }
}

// Switches the input box between a Date picker and a Month picker
function toggleDaybookInput() {
    const type = document.getElementById('daybook-filter-type').value;
    if (type === 'day') {
        document.getElementById('daybook-date').classList.remove('d-none');
        document.getElementById('daybook-month').classList.add('d-none');
        document.getElementById('voucher-log-title').innerText = "Daily Voucher Log";
    } else {
        document.getElementById('daybook-date').classList.add('d-none');
        document.getElementById('daybook-month').classList.remove('d-none');
        document.getElementById('voucher-log-title').innerText = "Monthly Voucher Register";
        
        // Set default month to current month if empty
        if(!document.getElementById('daybook-month').value) {
            const now = new Date();
            document.getElementById('daybook-month').value = now.getFullYear() + '-' + String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2, '0');
        }
    }
    generateDayBook();
}

// The Upgraded Generator Engine
function generateDayBook() {
    const filterType = document.getElementById('daybook-filter-type').value;
    const targetValue = filterType === 'day' ? document.getElementById('daybook-date').value : document.getElementById('daybook-month').value;
    
    if(!targetValue) return;

    let entries = [];
    let totalIn = 0;
    let totalOut = 0;

    // Helper to check if a record matches our filter
    const isMatch = (recordDate) => {
        if (!recordDate) return false;
        if (filterType === 'day') return recordDate === targetValue; // Exact day match (YYYY-MM-DD)
        return recordDate.startsWith(targetValue); // Month match (Starts with YYYY-MM)
    };

    // Scan Invoices (Sales)
    if (DATA && DATA.invoices) {
        DATA.invoices.forEach(inv => {
            let existingStatus = String(inv.status).trim();
            if (isMatch(inv.date) && !existingStatus.includes("CANCELLED") && inv.type === "Tax Invoice") {
                // 🛑 TALLY FIX: Sales Invoice is an OUTWARD flow of goods/value
                entries.push({ 
                    date: inv.date, 
                    type: 'Sales Invoice', 
                    badge: 'bg-primary', 
                    ref: inv.invNum, 
                    name: inv.company, 
                    inflow: 0, 
                    outflow: inv.total // Moves to Outward column
                });
            }
        });
    }

    // Scan Purchases
    if (DATA && DATA.purchases) {
        DATA.purchases.forEach(pur => {
            let existingStatus = String(pur.status).trim();
            if (isMatch(pur.date) && !existingStatus.includes("CANCELLED")) {
                // 🛑 TALLY FIX: Purchase Bill is an INWARD flow of goods/value
                entries.push({ 
                    date: pur.date, 
                    type: 'Purchase Bill', 
                    badge: 'bg-warning text-dark', 
                    ref: pur.invNo || pur.invNum || pur.billNo || "N/A", 
                    name: pur.vendor || pur.name || pur.company || pur.vendorName || "Unknown", 
                    inflow: pur.total, // Moves to Inward column
                    outflow: 0 
                });
            }
        });
    }

    // Scan Payments & Receipts
    if (DATA && DATA.payments) {
        DATA.payments.forEach(pay => {
            if (isMatch(pay.date) && String(pay.isDeleted).trim().toUpperCase() !== "TRUE") {
                if (pay.type === "Invoice" || pay.type === "Advance") { 
                    entries.push({ date: pay.date, type: 'Receipt', badge: 'bg-success', ref: pay.ref || "ADV", name: pay.client, inflow: pay.amount, outflow: 0 });
                } else { 
                    entries.push({ date: pay.date, type: 'Payment', badge: 'bg-danger', ref: pay.ref || "PAY", name: pay.client, inflow: 0, outflow: pay.amount });
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // Sort entries chronologically by date
    entries.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));

    // Render Table
    let html = '';
    entries.forEach(e => {
        totalIn += parseFloat(e.inflow) || 0;
        totalOut += parseFloat(e.outflow) || 0;
        
        // Only show the date column if we are in Monthly view
        let dateStr = filterType === 'month' ? `<td class="text-muted small">${e.date.split('-').reverse().join('-')}</td>` : '';

        html += `
        <tr>
            ${dateStr}
            <td><span class="badge ${e.badge} w-100 py-2">${e.type}</span></td>
            <td class="fw-bold text-muted">${e.ref}</td>
            <td class="fw-bold">${e.name}</td>
            <td class="text-end fw-bold text-success">${e.inflow > 0 ? '₹' + parseFloat(e.inflow).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) : '-'}</td>
            <td class="text-end fw-bold text-danger">${e.outflow > 0 ? '₹' + parseFloat(e.outflow).toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2}) : '-'}</td>
        </tr>`;
    });

    if (entries.length === 0) {
        let displayVal = filterType === 'day' ? targetValue.split('-').reverse().join('-') : targetValue;
        html = `<tr><td colspan="${filterType === 'month' ? '6' : '5'}" class="text-center text-muted py-4"><i class="bi bi-inbox fs-3 d-block mb-2"></i>No vouchers found for ${displayVal}</td></tr>`;
    }

    // Adjust table header dynamically
    let theadHtml = `
        <tr>
            ${filterType === 'month' ? '<th width="10%">Date</th>' : ''}
            <th width="15%">Voucher Type</th>
            <th width="15%">Voucher No.</th>
            <th width="${filterType === 'month' ? '30%' : '40%'}">Particulars</th>
            <th width="15%" class="text-end">Inward (₹)</th>
            <th width="15%" class="text-end">Outward (₹)</th>
        </tr>`;

    document.querySelector('#cb-tab-daybook thead').innerHTML = theadHtml;
    document.getElementById('daybook-tbody').innerHTML = html;
    document.getElementById('daybook-total-in').innerText = '₹' + totalIn.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
    document.getElementById('daybook-total-out').innerText = '₹' + totalOut.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
    // 🚀 NEW: Dynamically adjust the Footer ColSpan & Text based on view
    let footerLabelCell = document.querySelector('#cb-tab-daybook tfoot td:first-child');
    if (footerLabelCell) {
        if (filterType === 'month') {
            footerLabelCell.colSpan = 4; // 4 columns + 2 total columns = 6
            footerLabelCell.innerText = "Total Voucher Value For The Month:";
        } else {
            footerLabelCell.colSpan = 3; // 3 columns + 2 total columns = 5
            footerLabelCell.innerText = "Total Voucher Value For The Day:";
        }
    }
  }
  function saveVendor(e) {
    // 1. Prevent the page refresh illusion!
    if (e) e.preventDefault(); 
    
    const name = document.getElementById('v-name').value;
    const gstin = document.getElementById('v-gstin').value.toUpperCase();
    
    if (!name.trim()) return Swal.fire('Error', 'Vendor Name is required.', 'error');
    if (gstin && typeof validateGSTIN === 'function' && !validateGSTIN(gstin)) {
         return Swal.fire('Invalid GSTIN', 'Please check the GSTIN format.', 'error');
    }

    showLoad(); // Start spinner

    // 2. Build the payload based EXACTLY on your HTML modal
    const d = {
        id: document.getElementById('v-id').value, 
        name: name, 
        gstin: gstin, 
        phone: document.getElementById('v-phone').value, 
        email: document.getElementById('v-email').value, 
        address: document.getElementById('v-addr').value
    };

    // 3. Fetch Token for Security
    const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');

    // 4. Send to Backend
    google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler((r) => {
     hideLoad(); 
     
     let res;
     try { res = JSON.parse(r); } catch(err) { return Swal.fire('Error', 'Invalid server response', 'error'); }
     
     if (res.status === "error") return Swal.fire('Error', res.message, 'error');
     
     closeModal('modal-vendor'); 
     silentSync();                                   // ← Changed
     Toast.fire({icon:'success', title:'Vendor Saved'}); 
          }) 
        
        .withFailureHandler((err) => {
             hideLoad(); 
             Swal.fire('Server Error', err.message || 'Failed to save vendor.', 'error');
        })
        .saveVendor(token, JSON.stringify(d)); 
}
// ==========================================
// UNIVERSAL TABLE SORTER (High Performance)
// ==========================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
        th.style.cursor = 'pointer'; // Make it look clickable
        th.title = "Click to sort";
        
        th.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const table = th.closest('table');
            const tbody = table.querySelector('tbody');
            // Ignore the "Loading..." rows
            if (tbody.querySelector('td[colspan]')) return; 
            
            const rows = Array.from(tbody.querySelectorAll('tr'));
            const index = Array.from(th.parentNode.children).indexOf(th);
            const isAsc = th.classList.contains('asc');
            
            rows.sort((a, b) => {
                let valA = a.children[index].innerText.trim();
                let valB = b.children[index].innerText.trim();
                
                // Smart Currency/Number parsing (Strips ₹ and commas)
                let numA = parseFloat(valA.replace(/[^0-9.-]+/g, ""));
                let numB = parseFloat(valB.replace(/[^0-9.-]+/g, ""));
                
                if(!isNaN(numA) && !isNaN(numB) && valA.match(/[0-9]/) && valB.match(/[0-9]/)) {
                    return isAsc ? numA - numB : numB - numA;
                }
                // Fallback to alphabetical sorting
                return isAsc ? valA.localeCompare(valB) : valB.localeCompare(valA);
            });
            
            // Toggle arrow state
            th.closest('tr').querySelectorAll('th').forEach(h => h.classList.remove('asc', 'desc'));
            th.classList.add(isAsc ? 'desc' : 'asc');
            
            // Fast DOM update
            const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
            rows.forEach(row => fragment.appendChild(row));
            tbody.appendChild(fragment);
        });
    });
});

// PREMIUM CASHBOOK PRINTER
// ==========================================
function printCleanCashBook() {
    const closeBal = document.getElementById('cb-current-bal') ? document.getElementById('cb-current-bal').innerText : "₹0.00";
    const tableHtml = document.getElementById('tbl-cashbook-main').outerHTML;
    
    // 1. Remove any old hidden print frames
    let oldFrame = document.getElementById('hidden-print-frame');
    if (oldFrame) oldFrame.remove();

    // 2. Create the invisible iframe
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.id = 'hidden-print-frame';
    iframe.style.position = 'absolute';
    iframe.style.width = '0px';
    iframe.style.height = '0px';
    iframe.style.border = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(iframe);

    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });

    // 3. Write the Premium Cashbook into the hidden frame
    const doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
    doc.open();
    doc.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html>
        <head>
            <title>Cashbook Ledger</title>
            <style>
                /* Premium Print Typography */
                body { font-family: 'Helvetica Neue', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; padding: 0; color: #111; font-size: 12px; }
                @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 15mm; }
                
                /* Header Styling */
                .header-container { text-align: center; border-bottom: 2px solid #222; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
                .company-name { font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 5px 0; letter-spacing: 1px; }
                .report-title { font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #555; text-transform: uppercase; margin: 0; }
                
                /* Details Box */
                .details-box { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 20px; background-color: #f9f9f9; padding: 10px 15px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 4px; }
                .details-box h3, .details-box h4 { margin: 0; font-size: 14px; }
                .timestamp { font-size: 11px; color: #666; }

                /* Table Styling */
                table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 10px; }
                th, td { border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px 10px; }
                th { background-color: #f4f4f4 !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; font-weight: 600; text-align: left; color: #333; }
                tr { page-break-inside: avoid; }
                
                /* Alignments & Colors */
                .text-end { text-align: right; }
                .text-center { text-align: center; }
                .fw-bold { font-weight: bold; }
                .text-danger { color: #d32f2f !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }
                .text-success { color: #2e7d32 !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }

                /* HIDE UI ELEMENTS ON PRINT (Removes the Action column perfectly!) */
                button, .btn, th:last-child, td:last-child { display: none !important; }
                
                /* Footer */
                .footer { margin-top: 30px; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; color: #777; border-top: 1px solid #eee; padding-top: 10px; }
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <div class="header-container">
                <h1 class="company-name">SAI DURGA</h1>
                <h2 class="report-title">Cashbook Ledger</h2>
            </div>

            <div class="details-box">
                <div>
                    <h3 style="color:#333;">Account: <span style="color:#000;">Main Cashbook</span></h3>
                    <div class="timestamp">Generated on: ${today}</div>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: right;">
                    <h4 style="color:#555;">Closing Balance</h4>
                    <h3 style="font-size: 18px; color: #111;">${closeBal}</h3>
                </div>
            </div>

            ${tableHtml}
            
            <div class="footer">
                This is a computer-generated document from SD Dynamics. No signature is required.
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
    `);
    doc.close();

    // 4. Give the browser time to render, then print
    setTimeout(function() {
        iframe.contentWindow.focus();
        iframe.contentWindow.print();
    }, 500);
}
