// PREMIUM DAY BOOK PRINTER
// ==========================================
function printCleanDayBook() {
    // 1. FIXED: Now looks for exactly 'tbl-daybook-main'
    const tableElement = document.getElementById('tbl-daybook-main'); 
    const tableHtml = tableElement ? tableElement.outerHTML : "<p>No data available to print.</p>";
    
    // 2. FIXED: Grabs the correct Date or Month input from your Day Book HTML
    let reportDate = "Today";
    const viewType = document.getElementById('daybook-filter-type') ? document.getElementById('daybook-filter-type').value : 'day';
    
    if (viewType === 'day') {
        const dateInput = document.getElementById('daybook-date');
        if (dateInput && dateInput.value) {
            const d = new Date(dateInput.value);
            reportDate = d.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
        }
    } else {
        const monthInput = document.getElementById('daybook-month');
        if (monthInput && monthInput.value) {
            // Formats "2026-03" into "Mar 2026"
            const d = new Date(monthInput.value + "-01");
            reportDate = d.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
        }
    }

    // 3. Remove any old hidden print frames
    let oldFrame = document.getElementById('hidden-print-frame');
    if (oldFrame) oldFrame.remove();

    // 4. Create the invisible iframe
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.id = 'hidden-print-frame';
    iframe.style.position = 'absolute';
    iframe.style.width = '0px';
    iframe.style.height = '0px';
    iframe.style.border = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(iframe);

    const generatedOn = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

    // 5. Write the Premium Day Book into the hidden frame
    const doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
    doc.open();
    doc.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html>
        <head>
            <title>Day Book - ${reportDate}</title>
            <style>
                /* Premium Print Typography */
                body { font-family: 'Helvetica Neue', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; padding: 0; color: #111; font-size: 12px; }
                @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 15mm; }
                
                /* Header Styling */
                .header-container { text-align: center; border-bottom: 2px solid #222; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
                .company-name { font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 5px 0; letter-spacing: 1px; }
                .report-title { font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #555; text-transform: uppercase; margin: 0; }
                
                /* Details Box */
                .details-box { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 20px; background-color: #f9f9f9; padding: 10px 15px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 4px; }
                .details-box h3, .details-box h4 { margin: 0; font-size: 14px; }
                .timestamp { font-size: 11px; color: #666; }

                /* Table Styling */
                table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 10px; }
                th, td { border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px 10px; }
                th { background-color: #f4f4f4 !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; font-weight: 600; text-align: left; color: #333; }
                tr { page-break-inside: avoid; }
                
                /* Alignments & Colors */
                .text-end { text-align: right; }
                .text-center { text-align: center; }
                .fw-bold { font-weight: bold; }
                .text-danger { color: #d32f2f !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }
                .text-success { color: #2e7d32 !important; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; }

                /* HIDE UI ELEMENTS ON PRINT */
                button, .btn, .hide-print { display: none !important; }
                
                /* Footer */
                .footer { margin-top: 30px; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; color: #777; border-top: 1px solid #eee; padding-top: 10px; }
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <div class="header-container">
                <h1 class="company-name">SAI DURGA</h1>
                <h2 class="report-title">Voucher Log (Day Book)</h2>
            </div>

            <div class="details-box">
                <div>
                    <h3 style="color:#333;">Report Period: <span style="color:#000;">${reportDate}</span></h3>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: right;">
                    <div class="timestamp">Generated on: ${generatedOn}</div>
                </div>
            </div>

            ${tableHtml}
            
            <div class="footer">
                This is a computer-generated document from SD Dynamics. No signature is required.
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
    `);
    doc.close();

    // 6. Give the browser time to render, then print
    setTimeout(function() {
        iframe.contentWindow.focus();
        iframe.contentWindow.print();
    }, 500);
}
let pnlChartInstance = null;

function renderPnL() {
if (!DATA.payments) return;

let startD = document.getElementById('pnl-start').value;
let endD = document.getElementById('pnl-end').value;

let totalIn = 0;
let totalOut = 0;

// Crunch the numbers from the Cashbook
DATA.payments.forEach(p => {
let pDate = p.date.split('T')[0];
if (startD && pDate < startD) return; if (endD && pDate> endD) return;

  let amt = parseFloat(p.amount) || 0;
  if (p.type === "GST Payment" || p.type === "Vendor Payment" || p.type === "Expense") {
  totalOut += Math.abs(amt);
  } else {
  if (amt > 0) totalIn += amt;
  else totalOut += Math.abs(amt);
  }
  });

  let netProfit = totalIn - totalOut;
  let marginPct = totalIn > 0 ? ((netProfit / totalIn) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;

  // Update the UI Cards
  document.getElementById('pnl-revenue').innerText = '₹' + totalIn.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});
  document.getElementById('pnl-expenses').innerText = '₹' + totalOut.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits:
  2});
  document.getElementById('pnl-net').innerText = '₹' + netProfit.toLocaleString('en-IN', {minimumFractionDigits: 2});

  const marginBadge = document.getElementById('pnl-margin-pct');
  marginBadge.innerText = marginPct + '% Net Margin';
  marginBadge.className = netProfit >= 0 ? 'badge bg-success rounded-pill mt-2' : 'badge bg-danger rounded-pill mt-2';

  // ==========================================
  // CRASH-PROOF CHART RENDERER
  // ==========================================
  try {
      // 1. Declare the chart globally so it never loses memory context
      window.pnlChartInstance = window.pnlChartInstance || null;
      
      const canvasEl = document.getElementById('pnlChart');
      if (canvasEl) {
          const ctx = canvasEl.getContext('2d');
          
          // 2. Destroy the old chart securely before drawing a new one
          if (window.pnlChartInstance) {
              window.pnlChartInstance.destroy();
          }

          // 3. Draw the new chart with guaranteed safe numbers (|| 0 prevents NaN crashes)
          window.pnlChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
              type: 'doughnut',
              data: {
                  labels: ['Money In', 'Money Out'],
                  datasets: [{
                      data: [totalIn || 0, totalOut || 0],
                      backgroundColor: ['#198754', '#dc3545'], // Bootstrap Success & Danger colors
                      hoverOffset: 4,
                      borderWidth: 0
                  }]
              },
              options: {
                  responsive: true,
                  maintainAspectRatio: false,
                  cutout: '50%',
                  plugins: {
                      legend: { 
                          position: 'bottom',
                          labels: { font: { family: 'Inter', weight: 'bold' } }
                      }
                  }
              }
          });
      } else {
          console.warn("Could not find the canvas element with id 'pnlChart'");
      }
  } catch (err) {
      console.error("Chart rendering crashed, but the system caught it:", err);
  }
  }
 // ==========================================
  // SILENT BACKGROUND SYNC (No Flicker)
  // ==========================================
  function silentSync(callback) {
      const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sessionToken');
      if (!token) return;

      google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
              try {
                  const response = JSON.parse(res);
                  if (response.status === "success" && response.data) {
                      DATA = response.data; // Update global memory
                      
                      // 🚀 Instantly Redraw Everything using your existing functions!
                      render(); 
                      renderCFODashboard();
                      if (typeof renderReconTable === "function") renderReconTable();
                      if (typeof renderCharts === "function") renderCharts();
                      populateDatalists(); 
                      populateFilters(); 
                      loadConfig(); 
                      
                      // Keep Active Tabs Alive
                      if(document.getElementById('view-cashbook').classList.contains('active')) renderCashBook();
                      if(document.getElementById('view-reports').classList.contains('active')) {
                          if(document.getElementById('rep-sec-fintb').style.display !== 'none') renderFinancials();
                          if(document.getElementById('rep-sec-finbs').style.display !== 'none') renderFinancials();
                      }

                      if (typeof callback === 'function') callback();
                  }
              } catch (e) {
                  console.error("Silent sync failed", e);
              }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
              console.error("Silent sync error:", err);
          })
          .getAppData(token);
  }

function renderAllTables() {
    if (typeof renderInvoices === 'function') renderInvoices();
    if (typeof renderClients === 'function') renderClients();
    if (typeof renderVendors === 'function') renderVendors();
    if (typeof renderWorkOrders === 'function') renderWorkOrders();
    if (typeof renderPurchases === 'function') renderPurchases();
    if (typeof renderPayments === 'function') renderPayments();
    // Add any other render functions you have (e.g. renderDayBook, renderLedger)
}

// ==========================================
    // AUTO-FILL SITE & COST HEAD FROM WORK ORDER
    // ==========================================
    function autoFillExpenseWO(formPrefix) {
        // formPrefix will be either 'pur' (Purchases) or 'pay' (Payments)
        const woSelect = document.getElementById(formPrefix + '-wo');
        if (!woSelect || !woSelect.value) return;

        // 1. Find the selected Work Order data
        const selectedWO = DATA.workOrders.find(w => w.woNo === woSelect.value);
        if (!selectedWO) return;

        // 2. Auto-fill the Site
        const siteSelect = document.getElementById(formPrefix + '-site');
        if (siteSelect) {
            for (let i = 0; i < siteSelect.options.length; i++) {
                if (siteSelect.options[i].value === selectedWO.site) {
                    siteSelect.selectedIndex = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Smart Auto-fill Cost Head from actual WO Items
        const headInput = document.getElementById(formPrefix + '-costhead') || document.getElementById(formPrefix + '-head');
        if (headInput) {
            let suggestedHead = "";
            
            try {
                // Safely convert items from String to Array if needed
                if (selectedWO.items) {
                    let itemsArray = typeof selectedWO.items === 'string' ? JSON.parse(selectedWO.items) : selectedWO.items;
                    
                    if (Array.isArray(itemsArray) && itemsArray.length > 0) {
                        const validItem = itemsArray.find(i => (i.costHead || i.CostHead || i.head) && String(i.costHead || i.CostHead || i.head).trim() !== "");
                        if (validItem) {
                            suggestedHead = validItem.costHead || validItem.CostHead || validItem.head;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Could not parse WO items for Cost Head auto-fill", e);
            }
            
            // 🚀 THE FIX: Always forcefully apply the new Cost Head!
            if (suggestedHead) {
                headInput.value = suggestedHead; // Overwrites whatever was there before
            } else if (headInput.value.trim() === "") {
                headInput.value = "Project Material"; // Fallback only if no items existed
            }
            
            headInput.focus();
        }
    }
    // ==========================================
// 3. FIX: GST B2B/B2C BLUE TAB BUG
// ==========================================
function switchSubRepTab(type) {
    // Hide both tables safely
    const b2b = document.getElementById('gst-b2b');
    const b2c = document.getElementById('gst-b2c');
    if(b2b) b2b.style.display = 'none';
    if(b2c) b2c.style.display = 'none';

    // Remove the blue 'active' class from BOTH tabs
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#rep-sec-gst .nav-link');
    tabs.forEach(t => {
        t.classList.remove('active');
        t.classList.remove('shadow-sm'); 
    });

    // Show only the clicked tab and make it blue
    if (type === 'b2b') {
        if(b2b) b2b.style.display = 'block';
        if(tabs[0]) tabs[0].classList.add('active', 'shadow-sm');
    } else {
        if(b2c) b2c.style.display = 'block';
        if(tabs[1]) tabs[1].classList.add('active', 'shadow-sm');
    }
}
  </script>
